     1	// The host validator (PLAN.md, section 3). A prediction is accepted only if it is exactly the schema, restates the envelope's one
     2	// frozen task verbatim, and its task converts to operations the existing runner validator accepts. Invalid proposals are counted
     3	// and scored as non-answers; nothing is ever repaired. The sham ({kind:"none"}) is an explicit branch that maps to NO scheduled
     4	// operation (the existing replay control omits the schedule), never to a radius-0 lesion or a zero feed.
     5	import { cellCount, worldW } from "@bl/schema";
     6	import { interventionsError, validateInterventions, type ScheduledIntervention } from "@bl/runner";
     7	import { canonical } from "../propose.ts";
     8	import { CLASSES, ENVELOPE_VERSION, PREDICTION_VERSION, envelopeIntact, type Direction3, type PredictionEnvelope, type Task } from "./envelope.ts";
     9	
    10	export interface Probs {
    11	  up: number;
    12	  down: number;
    13	  none: number;
    14	}
    15	export interface Prediction {
    16	  version: number;
    17	  caseId: string;
    18	  envelopeHash: string;
    19	  task: Task;
    20	  probs?: Probs;
    21	  abstain: boolean;
    22	  reason?: string;
    23	}
    24	export interface Validation {
    25	  ok: boolean;
    26	  errors: string[];
    27	}
    28	
    29	const PRED_KEYS = ["version", "caseId", "envelopeHash", "task", "probs", "abstain", "reason"];
    30	/**
    31	 * True when `v` is plain JSON data all the way down: plain records and arrays whose every property is an own, enumerable data
    32	 * property (no getters, setters, symbols or hidden non-enumerable fields), so JSON.stringify sees exactly what the validator saw.
    33	 */
    34	function plainData(v: unknown, depth = 0): boolean {
    35	  if (v === null || typeof v === "string" || typeof v === "boolean") return true;
    36	  if (typeof v === "number") return Number.isFinite(v);
    37	  if (depth > 8 || typeof v !== "object") return false;
    38	  const isArr = Array.isArray(v);
    39	  if (!isArr) {
    40	    const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);
    41	    if (proto !== Object.prototype && proto !== null) return false;
    42	  }
    43	  if (Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(v).length) return false;
    44	  for (const k of Reflect.ownKeys(v)) {
    45	    if (isArr && k === "length") continue;
    46	    const d = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v, k)!;
    47	    if (!d.enumerable || !("value" in d) || !plainData(d.value, depth + 1)) return false;
    48	  }
    49	  return true;
    50	}
    51	const FORBIDDEN = ["__proto__", "constructor", "prototype"];
    52	const isObj = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
    53	/** A JSON-compatible record: a plain object (Object.prototype or null prototype), so every field it has is its own and survives JSON.stringify. */
    54	const isPlain = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => {
    55	  if (!isObj(v)) return false;
    56	  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);
    57	  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null;
    58	};
    59	
    60	/** The scheduled operations a task asks for: null for the sham, else the list `validateInterventions` takes. Errors name the task, never repair it. */
    61	export function taskSchedule(env: PredictionEnvelope): { schedule: ScheduledIntervention[] | null; errors: string[] } {
    62	  const t = env.task, op = t.op, cfg = env.config, errors: string[] = [];
    63	  const W = worldW(cfg), H = cellCount(cfg) / W;
    64	  const entry = env.menu.ops.find((m) => m.kind === op.kind);
    65	  if (!entry) return { schedule: null, errors: [`operation ${JSON.stringify(op.kind)} is not on the menu`] };
    66	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(t.delta) || t.delta < env.menu.deltaMin || t.delta > env.menu.deltaMax) errors.push(`delta ${t.delta} is outside the menu's ${env.menu.deltaMin}..${env.menu.deltaMax}`);
    67	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(t.horizon) || t.horizon < t.delta) errors.push(`horizon ${t.horizon} must be at least delta ${t.delta}`);
    68	  if (op.kind === "none") {
    69	    for (const k of Object.keys(op)) if (k !== "kind") errors.push(`a sham has no key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    70	    return { schedule: null, errors };
    71	  }
    72	  const want = op.kind === "lesion" ? ["kind", "x", "y", "r"] : ["kind", "x", "y", "r", "amount"];
    73	  for (const k of Object.keys(op)) if (!want.includes(k)) errors.push(`a ${op.kind} has no key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    74	  for (const k of want) if (!Object.hasOwn(op, k)) errors.push(`a ${op.kind} needs the key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
    75	  if (errors.length) return { schedule: null, errors };
    76	  const { x, y, r } = op as { x: number; y: number; r: number };
    77	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(r) || r < (entry.rMin ?? 1) || r > (entry.rMax ?? 0)) errors.push(`radius ${r} is outside the menu's ${entry.rMin}..${entry.rMax}`);
    78	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(x) || x < 0 || x >= W) errors.push(`x ${x} is outside the world`);
    79	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(y) || y < 0 || y >= H) errors.push(`y ${y} is outside the world`);
    80	  let entryOut: ScheduledIntervention;
    81	  const step = t.forkStep + t.delta;
    82	  if (op.kind === "lesion") entryOut = { step, kind: "lesion", x, y, r };
    83	  else {
    84	    const a = op.amount as number;
    85	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(a) || a < (entry.amountMin ?? 1) || a > (entry.amountMax ?? 0)) errors.push(`amount ${a} is outside the menu's ${entry.amountMin}..${entry.amountMax}`);
    86	    entryOut = { step, kind: "feed", x, y, r, amount: op.kind === "drain" ? -a : a };
    87	  }
    88	  if (errors.length) return { schedule: null, errors };
    89	  const schedule = [entryOut];
    90	  const bad = validateInterventions(schedule);
    91	  if (bad.length) return { schedule: null, errors: bad };
    92	  const world = interventionsError({ interventions: schedule }, cfg, t.forkStep);
    93	  if (world) return { schedule: null, errors: [world] };
    94	  return { schedule, errors: [] };
    95	}
    96	
    97	/** An envelope must itself be sound: right version, intact hash, a task its own menu allows. Builders assert this; the validator trusts only a sound one. */
    98	export function validateEnvelope(env: PredictionEnvelope): Validation {
    99	  const errors: string[] = [];
   100	  if (env.version !== ENVELOPE_VERSION) errors.push(`envelope version ${env.version}`);
   101	  if (!envelopeIntact(env)) errors.push("envelope hash does not match its content");
   102	  errors.push(...taskSchedule(env).errors);
   103	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
   104	}
   105	
   106	export function validatePrediction(input: unknown, env: PredictionEnvelope): Validation {
   107	  const errors: string[] = [];
   108	  if (!isPlain(input)) return { ok: false, errors: ["a prediction must be a plain JSON object (no inherited fields)"] };
   109	  // Every field must be an own, enumerable data property (nothing hidden from JSON.stringify), and the prediction is then judged
   110	  // on its serialized snapshot, not on live objects.
   111	  if (!plainData(input)) return { ok: false, errors: ["a prediction must be plain data: own enumerable data properties only (no getters, hidden or inherited fields, symbols)"] };
   112	  const p = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(input)) as Record<string, unknown>;
   113	  for (const k of Object.keys(p)) {
   114	    if (FORBIDDEN.includes(k)) errors.push(`forbidden key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
   115	    else if (!PRED_KEYS.includes(k)) errors.push(`unknown key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
   116	  }
   117	  if (errors.length) return { ok: false, errors };
   118	  // Required fields must be the object's own: an inherited field would validate but serialize as absent.
   119	  for (const k of ["version", "caseId", "envelopeHash", "task", "abstain"]) if (!Object.hasOwn(p, k)) errors.push(`missing own field ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
   120	  if (errors.length) return { ok: false, errors };
   121	  let stable = false;
   122	  try {
   123	    stable = canonical(input) === canonical(p);
   124	  } catch {
   125	    stable = false;
   126	  }
   127	  if (!stable) return { ok: false, errors: ["the prediction does not survive JSON serialization unchanged"] };
   128	  if (p.version !== PREDICTION_VERSION) errors.push(`version must be ${PREDICTION_VERSION}`);
   129	  if (p.caseId !== env.caseId) errors.push(`caseId ${JSON.stringify(p.caseId)} is not ${JSON.stringify(env.caseId)}`);
   130	  if (p.envelopeHash !== env.envelopeHash) errors.push("envelopeHash does not match the envelope");
   131	  if (!isPlain(p.task)) errors.push("task is missing or not a plain object");
   132	  else {
   133	    let same = false;
   134	    try {
   135	      same = canonical(p.task) === canonical(env.task);
   136	    } catch {
   137	      same = false;
   138	    }
   139	    if (!same) errors.push("task does not restate the envelope's task verbatim (the question is fixed by the host)");
   140	  }
   141	  if (typeof p.abstain !== "boolean") errors.push("abstain must be a boolean");
   142	  if (p.reason !== undefined && typeof p.reason !== "string") errors.push("reason must be a string");
   143	  if (p.abstain === true) {
   144	    if (Object.hasOwn(p, "probs")) errors.push("an abstention carries no probs");
   145	  } else if (p.abstain === false) {
   146	    if (!isPlain(p.probs)) errors.push("probs are required unless abstaining, as a plain object");
   147	    else {
   148	      const pr = p.probs;
   149	      for (const k of Object.keys(pr)) if (!(CLASSES as readonly string[]).includes(k)) errors.push(`probs has unknown key ${JSON.stringify(k)}`);
   150	      let sum = 0;
   151	      for (const c of CLASSES) {
   152	        const v = Object.hasOwn(pr, c) ? pr[c] : undefined;
   153	        if (!Number.isInteger(v) || (v as number) < 0 || (v as number) > 1000) errors.push(`probs.${c} must be an integer in 0..1000 (per-mille)`);
   154	        else sum += v as number;
   155	      }
   156	      if (errors.length === 0 && sum !== 1000) errors.push(`probs must sum to exactly 1000, got ${sum}`);
   157	    }
   158	  }
   159	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
   160	}
   161	
   162	/** argmax of the stated probs, tie order none > down > up (derived, never submitted). */
   163	export function argmax(p: Probs): Direction3 {
   164	  let best: Direction3 = "none";
   165	  for (const c of ["none", "down", "up"] as const) if (p[c] > p[best]) best = c;
   166	  return best;
   167	}
   168	
   169	export interface FileCheck {
   170	  /** caseId -> prediction-or-null (null: invalid, or no valid line). */
   171	  byCase: Map<string, { prediction: Prediction | null; errors: string[] }>;
   172	  invalid: number;
   173	  missing: string[];
   174	  extra: string[];
   175	  duplicates: string[];
   176	  notObject: number;
   177	  /** Envelopes that fail validateEnvelope (version, recomputed hash, task on its own menu): their cases cannot be answered validly. */
   178	  badEnvelopes: string[];
   179	}
   180	
   181	/** File-level validation: exactly one line per case. Extras, duplicates and missing cases are counted; a duplicated case scores as invalid. */
   182	export function validatePredictionFile(lines: string[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): FileCheck {
   183	  const byEnv = new Map(envelopes.map((e) => [e.caseId, e]));
   184	  const out: FileCheck = { byCase: new Map(), invalid: 0, missing: [], extra: [], duplicates: [], notObject: 0, badEnvelopes: [] };
   185	  const unsound = new Map<string, string[]>();
   186	  for (const e of envelopes) {
   187	    const v = validateEnvelope(e);
   188	    if (!v.ok) {
   189	      unsound.set(e.caseId, v.errors);
   190	      out.badEnvelopes.push(e.caseId);
   191	    }
   192	  }
   193	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   194	  for (const line of lines) {
   195	    if (line.trim() === "") continue;
   196	    let raw: unknown;
   197	    try {
   198	      raw = JSON.parse(line);
   199	    } catch {
   200	      out.notObject++;
   201	      out.invalid++;
   202	      continue;
   203	    }
   204	    if (!isPlain(raw)) {
   205	      out.notObject++;
   206	      out.invalid++;
   207	      continue;
   208	    }
   209	    const id = typeof raw.caseId === "string" ? raw.caseId : "";
   210	    const env = byEnv.get(id);
   211	    if (!env) {
   212	      out.extra.push(id);
   213	      out.invalid++;
   214	      continue;
   215	    }
   216	    if (seen.has(id)) {
   217	      out.duplicates.push(id);
   218	      out.byCase.set(id, { prediction: null, errors: ["duplicate line for this case"] });
   219	      out.invalid++;
   220	      continue;
   221	    }
   222	    seen.add(id);
   223	    const v = unsound.has(id) ? { ok: false, errors: ["the envelope is unsound: " + unsound.get(id)!.join("; ")] } : validatePrediction(raw, env);
   224	    if (!v.ok) out.invalid++;
   225	    out.byCase.set(id, { prediction: v.ok ? (raw as unknown as Prediction) : null, errors: v.errors });
   226	  }
   227	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!seen.has(e.caseId)) out.missing.push(e.caseId);
   228	  return out;
   229	}
     1	// The transitive local source closure of an entry file: every repo file reachable through relative imports and `@bl/*` aliases.
     2	// meta.json's source hash covers all of it, so a change to the simulator, schema, metrics or a helper the harness runs changes the hash.
     3	import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
     4	import { dirname, join, relative, resolve } from "node:path";
     5	import { sha256Text } from "../propose.ts";
     6	
     7	const SPEC = /(?:^|\n)\s*(?:import|export)\s[^"'`;]*?from\s*["']([^"']+)["']|(?:^|\n)\s*import\s*["']([^"']+)["']|\bimport\(\s*["']([^"']+)["']\s*\)/g;
     8	
     9	/** Resolves an import specifier from `fromFile` to a repo file, or null for an external/builtin module. */
    10	export function resolveLocal(root: string, fromFile: string, spec: string): string | null {
    11	  let base: string;
    12	  if (spec.startsWith("./") || spec.startsWith("../")) base = resolve(dirname(fromFile), spec);
    13	  else if (spec.startsWith("@bl/")) base = join(root, "packages", spec.slice(4), "src/index.ts");
    14	  else return null;
    15	  for (const c of [base, base + ".ts", join(base, "index.ts")]) if (existsSync(c) && /\.(ts|js|mjs)$/.test(c)) return c;
    16	  throw new Error(`cannot resolve ${JSON.stringify(spec)} from ${relative(root, fromFile)}`);
    17	}
    18	
    19	/** Repo-relative paths of the closure, sorted. */
    20	export function sourceClosure(root: string, entries: readonly string[]): string[] {
    21	  const seen = new Set<string>();
    22	  const stack = entries.map((e) => resolve(root, e));
    23	  while (stack.length) {
    24	    const f = stack.pop()!;
    25	    if (seen.has(f)) continue;
    26	    seen.add(f);
    27	    const text = readFileSync(f, "utf8");
    28	    for (const m of text.matchAll(SPEC)) {
    29	      const spec = m[1] ?? m[2] ?? m[3];
    30	      const r = spec ? resolveLocal(root, f, spec) : null;
    31	      if (r && !seen.has(r)) stack.push(r);
    32	    }
    33	  }
    34	  return [...seen].map((f) => relative(root, f)).sort();
    35	}
    36	
    37	/** Resolver configuration and dependency identities that decide what a specifier means: hashed alongside the sources. */
    38	export const RESOLVER_CONFIG_FILES = ["deno.json", "package.json", "tsconfig.json"] as const;
    39	
    40	/** Bare (non-local) specifiers the closure imports, e.g. `node:fs` or `fflate`: recorded so a silently skipped external is still named. */
    41	export function externalSpecifiers(root: string, files: readonly string[]): string[] {
    42	  const out = new Set<string>();
    43	  for (const f of files) {
    44	    for (const m of readFileSync(join(root, f), "utf8").matchAll(SPEC)) {
    45	      const spec = m[1] ?? m[2] ?? m[3];
    46	      if (spec && !spec.startsWith("./") && !spec.startsWith("../") && !spec.startsWith("@bl/")) out.add(spec);
    47	    }
    48	  }
    49	  return [...out].sort();
    50	}
    51	
    52	/**
    53	 * The hash covers the closure's files, the resolver configuration (deno.json import map and npm pins, package.json, tsconfig.json
    54	 * paths) and the sorted list of external specifiers, so changing what `@bl/*` or `fflate` resolves to changes the hash.
    55	 */
    56	export function sourceClosureHash(root: string, entries: readonly string[]): { sha256: string; files: string[]; config: string[]; externals: string[] } {
    57	  const files = sourceClosure(root, entries);
    58	  const config = RESOLVER_CONFIG_FILES.filter((c) => existsSync(join(root, c)));
    59	  const externals = externalSpecifiers(root, files);
    60	  const parts = [...files, ...config].map((f) => `${f}\n${sha256Text(readFileSync(join(root, f), "utf8"))}`);
    61	  return { sha256: sha256Text([...parts, `externals\n${externals.join(",")}`].join("\n")), files, config, externals };
    62	}
     1	// The prediction-envelope harness CLI (docs/prediction-envelope-2026-11-01/PLAN.md). Deno, native CPU only: no GPU, no model call,
     2	// no network. Observer and tooling only; nothing here changes the dynamics.
     3	//
     4	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts protocol                       write the draft experiments/prediction-envelope/protocol.json (never overwrites)
     5	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage dev --out DIR      dev families only: DIR/dev/qualification.json (raw effects, tallies, stability)
     6	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts freeze --min-effect N [--out DIR]   set minEffect and devPrior from DIR/dev/qualification.json, write the freeze hash
     7	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage confirm --out DIR  all families; refuses unless protocol.json and protocol.freeze.sha256 are COMMITTED (HEAD) and match; `--exploratory yes` (last) skips the commit gate and marks the output EXPLORATORY
     8	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage smoke --protocol P --out DIR   a small protocol, no freeze check (output marked SMOKE in meta.json)
     9	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts validate FILE [--envelopes F]  check a predictions file against its envelopes (wrapped {proposer, prediction} or bare lines)
    10	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts report --out DIR               re-render report.html from the files in DIR
    11	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
    12	import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    13	import { cpus, platform, arch } from "node:os";
    14	import { dirname, join, relative, resolve } from "node:path";
    15	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    16	import { canonical, sha256Text } from "./lib/propose.ts";
    17	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
    18	import { CANONICAL_FILES, buildOutputs, rerender } from "./lib/prediction/output.ts";
    19	import { ORACLE_IDS, RUN_VERSIONS, confirmRefusal, draftProtocol, freezeRefusal, generate, priorFromEffects, protocolHash, qualificationReport, splitPlanOf, type Protocol } from "./lib/prediction/run.ts";
    20	import { validatePredictionFile } from "./lib/prediction/validate.ts";
    21	import { sourceClosureHash as closureHash } from "./lib/prediction/closure.ts";
    22	
    23	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    24	const root = resolve(here, "..");
    25	const EXP = join(root, "experiments/prediction-envelope");
    26	const PROTOCOL = join(EXP, "protocol.json");
    27	const FREEZE = join(EXP, "protocol.freeze.sha256");
    28	
    29	function args(argv: string[]): { cmd: string; pos: string[]; opt: Record<string, string> } {
    30	  const [cmd = "", ...rest] = argv;
    31	  const pos: string[] = [], opt: Record<string, string> = {};
    32	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
    33	    if (rest[i].startsWith("--")) {
    34	      opt[rest[i].slice(2)] = rest[i + 1] ?? "";
    35	      i++;
    36	    } else pos.push(rest[i]);
    37	  }
    38	  return { cmd, pos, opt };
    39	}
    40	const readProtocol = (path: string): Protocol => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Protocol;
    41	const writeText = (path: string, text: string): void => {
    42	  mkdirSync(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
    43	  writeFileSync(path, text);
    44	};
    45	function sh(cmd: string, a: string[]): string {
    46	  try {
    47	    return execFileSync(cmd, a, { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] }).trim();
    48	  } catch {
    49	    return "unknown";
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	const CLOSURE_ENTRIES = ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"];
    53	const sourceClosureHash = (): string => closureHash(root, CLOSURE_ENTRIES).sha256;
    54	/** The committed (HEAD) text of a repo path, or null when HEAD has no such file. */
    55	function committedText(path: string): string | null {
    56	  try {
    57	    return execFileSync("git", ["show", `HEAD:${relative(root, path)}`], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] });
    58	  } catch {
    59	    return null;
    60	  }
    61	}
    62	const readJsonl = <T>(path: string): T[] => readFileSync(path, "utf8").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as T);
    63	
    64	function main(): number {
    65	  const { cmd, pos, opt } = args(Deno.args);
    66	  if (cmd === "protocol") {
    67	    if (existsSync(PROTOCOL)) {
    68	      console.error(`${PROTOCOL} exists; not overwriting`);
    69	      return 1;
    70	    }
    71	    writeText(PROTOCOL, JSON.stringify(draftProtocol(), null, 2) + "\n");
    72	    console.log(`wrote ${PROTOCOL}`);
    73	    return 0;
    74	  }
    75	  if (cmd === "run") {
    76	    const stage = opt.stage ?? "";
    77	    const out = resolve(opt.out ?? "");
    78	    if (!opt.out) throw new Error("--out is required");
    79	    const protoPath = stage === "smoke" ? resolve(opt.protocol ?? "") : PROTOCOL;
    80	    const p = readProtocol(protoPath);
    81	    const t0 = Date.now();
    82	    if (stage === "dev") {
    83	      if (p.frozen) throw new Error("the protocol is already frozen; the dev stage is for qualification before the freeze");
    84	      const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    85	      const gen = generate(p, plan.dev, true);
    86	      writeText(join(out, "dev/qualification.json"), JSON.stringify(qualificationReport(p, gen), null, 1) + "\n");
    87	      console.log(`dev: ${gen.results.length} families, ${gen.variants.length} seed variants, ${Date.now() - t0} ms -> ${join(out, "dev/qualification.json")}`);
    88	      return 0;
    89	    }
    90	    if (stage === "confirm" || stage === "smoke") {
    91	      // `--exploratory` runs the confirm computation without the committed-freeze gate and marks every output EXPLORATORY: the test
    92	      // families are then not held out (their outcomes may already have been seen), so the run is a rehearsal, not a confirmation.
    93	      const exploratory = stage === "confirm" && opt.exploratory !== undefined;
    94	      if (stage === "confirm" && !exploratory) {
    95	        const freezeWorking = existsSync(FREEZE) ? readFileSync(FREEZE, "utf8") : null;
    96	        const refusal = confirmRefusal(p, freezeWorking, { protocol: committedText(PROTOCOL), freeze: committedText(FREEZE), protocolWorking: readFileSync(PROTOCOL, "utf8"), freezeWorking: freezeWorking ?? "" });
    97	        if (refusal) throw new Error(`refusing to confirm: ${refusal}`);
    98	      }
    99	      if (stage === "confirm" && exploratory) {
   100	        const refusal = freezeRefusal(p, existsSync(FREEZE) ? readFileSync(FREEZE, "utf8") : null);
   101	        if (refusal) throw new Error(`refusing even to rehearse: ${refusal}`);
   102	      }
   103	      const gen = generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true);
   104	      const built = buildOutputs(p, gen);
   105	      for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(built.files)) writeText(join(out, name), text);
   106	      const meta = {
   107	        stage,
   108	        status: stage === "smoke" ? "SMOKE" : exploratory ? "EXPLORATORY" : "CONFIRM",
   109	        ...(exploratory ? { exploratoryReason: "run before the freeze was committed, with test outcomes already inspected; not a held-out confirmation" } : {}),
   110	        gitSha: sh("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
   111	        gitDirty: sh("git", ["status", "--porcelain", "--", "tools", "packages", "experiments/prediction-envelope", "deno.json", "package.json", "tsconfig.json"]) !== "",
   112	        sourceClosureSha256: sourceClosureHash(),
   113	        versions: RUN_VERSIONS,
   114	        protocolSha256: protocolHash(p),
   115	        command: `deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts ${Deno.args.join(" ")}`,
   116	        hardware: { cpu: cpus()[0]?.model ?? "unknown", cores: cpus().length, platform: platform(), arch: arch() },
   117	        wallMs: Date.now() - t0,
   118	        cellSteps: built.comparison.validNonNullYieldAndCost.totalCellSteps,
   119	        statement: "CPU only (sim-ref). No model call, no GPU, no network, no paid compute.",
   120	      };
   121	      writeText(join(out, "meta.json"), JSON.stringify(meta, null, 2) + "\n");
   122	      console.log(`${stage}: ${gen.results.length} families, ${built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].split("\n").length - 1} cases, ${Date.now() - t0} ms -> ${out}`);
   123	      return 0;
   124	    }
   125	    throw new Error(`unknown --stage ${JSON.stringify(stage)} (dev, confirm, smoke)`);
   126	  }
   127	  if (cmd === "freeze") {
   128	    const out = resolve(opt.out ?? join(EXP, "v0"));
   129	    const min = Number(opt["min-effect"]);
   130	    if (!Number.isFinite(min) || min <= 0) throw new Error("--min-effect must be a positive number");
   131	    const p = readProtocol(PROTOCOL);
   132	    if (p.frozen) throw new Error("already frozen");
   133	    const q = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out, "dev/qualification.json"), "utf8")) as { protocolHash: string; devEffects: { bodyMass: number }[] };
   134	    if (q.protocolHash !== protocolHash(p)) throw new Error("dev/qualification.json was made with a different draft protocol");
   135	    p.minEffect = min;
   136	    p.devPrior = priorFromEffects(q.devEffects.map((e) => e.bodyMass), min);
   137	    p.frozen = true;
   138	    writeText(PROTOCOL, JSON.stringify(p, null, 2) + "\n");
   139	    writeText(FREEZE, protocolHash(p) + "\n");
   140	    console.log(`frozen: minEffect ${min}, devPrior ${JSON.stringify(p.devPrior)}, hash ${protocolHash(p)}`);
   141	    return 0;
   142	  }
   143	  if (cmd === "validate") {
   144	    const file = resolve(pos[0] ?? "");
   145	    const envPath = resolve(opt.envelopes ?? join(dirname(file), "envelopes.jsonl"));
   146	    const envelopes = readJsonl<PredictionEnvelope>(envPath);
   147	    const lines = readFileSync(file, "utf8").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "");
   148	    const groups = new Map<string, string[]>();
   149	    for (const l of lines) {
   150	      let proposer = "(bare)", body = l;
   151	      try {
   152	        const o = JSON.parse(l) as { proposer?: unknown; prediction?: unknown };
   153	        if (o && typeof o === "object" && typeof o.proposer === "string" && "prediction" in o) {
   154	          proposer = o.proposer;
   155	          body = JSON.stringify(o.prediction);
   156	        }
   157	      } catch {
   158	        /* the validator counts it */
   159	      }
   160	      (groups.get(proposer) ?? groups.set(proposer, []).get(proposer)!).push(body);
   161	    }
   162	    // The dev-only oracle is validated against dev envelopes only, when the protocol snapshot beside the file says which are dev.
   163	    const snap = join(dirname(file), "protocol.snapshot.json");
   164	    const devFamilies = existsSync(snap) ? new Set(splitPlanOf(readProtocol(snap)).dev) : null;
   165	    // An empty submission is still checked against the envelopes: every case is missing.
   166	    if (groups.size === 0) groups.set("(empty file)", []);
   167	    let bad = 0;
   168	    for (const [id, ls] of groups) {
   169	      const meant = devFamilies && ORACLE_IDS.includes(id) ? envelopes.filter((e) => devFamilies.has(e.familyId)) : envelopes;
   170	      const r = validatePredictionFile(ls, meant);
   171	      bad += r.invalid + r.missing.length + r.extra.length + r.duplicates.length + r.notObject + r.badEnvelopes.length;
   172	      console.log(`${id}: ${ls.length} lines, invalid ${r.invalid}, missing ${r.missing.length}${r.missing.length ? ` (${r.missing.slice(0, 5).join(", ")}${r.missing.length > 5 ? ", ..." : ""})` : ""}, extra ${r.extra.length}, duplicates ${r.duplicates.length}, unsound envelopes ${r.badEnvelopes.length}`);
   173	    }
   174	    return bad === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   175	  }
   176	  if (cmd === "report") {
   177	    // Re-renders comparison.md, report.html and canonical.sha256 from the canonical data files in DIR, and refreshes the source hash in meta.json.
   178	    if (!opt.out) throw new Error("--out is required");
   179	    const dir = resolve(opt.out);
   180	    const derived = rerender((name) => readFileSync(join(dir, name), "utf8"));
   181	    for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(derived)) writeText(join(dir, name), text);
   182	    const metaPath = join(dir, "meta.json");
   183	    if (existsSync(metaPath)) {
   184	      const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(metaPath, "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>;
   185	      // The generation hash stays as recorded (it names the code that made the outcomes); the renderer's hash is recorded separately.
   186	      meta.reportRenderedFromCanonicalData = { sourceClosureSha256: sourceClosureHash() };
   187	      writeText(metaPath, JSON.stringify(meta, null, 2) + "\n");
   188	    }
   189	    console.log(`report: re-rendered ${Object.keys(derived).join(", ")} in ${dir}`);
   190	    return 0;
   191	  }
   192	  console.error("usage: predict.ts protocol | run --stage dev|confirm|smoke --out DIR | freeze --min-effect N | validate FILE | report --out DIR");
   193	  return 2;
   194	}
   195	
   196	try {
   197	  Deno.exit(main());
   198	} catch (e) {
   199	  console.error((e as Error).message);
   200	  Deno.exit(1);
   201	}
   202	void CANONICAL_FILES;
   203	void canonical;

     1	// Spawned `deno run --no-lock` smoke test of the CLI: a tiny smoke run, then `validate` on its predictions, then the confirm refusals.
     2	import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
     3	import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
     4	import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
     5	import { join } from "node:path";
     6	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
     7	import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

     9	import { donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
    10	import { tinyProtocol } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";
    11	
    12	const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
    13	const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "predict-cli-"));
    14	afterAll(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
    15	const deno = (args: string[]) => spawnSync("deno", ["run", "--no-lock", "-A", "tools/predict.ts", ...args], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" });
    16	
    17	describe("tools/predict.ts", () => {
    18	  const p = tinyProtocol();
    19	  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 4);
    20	  p.nDev = 2;
    21	  p.nQualify = 1;
    22	  p.donors = donorsFor(splitPlanOf(p), p.salt);
    23	  const protoPath = join(dir, "protocol.json");
    24	  writeFileSync(protoPath, JSON.stringify(p));
    25	
    26	  it("runs a tiny smoke stage, writes the canonical files, and validate accepts its predictions", () => {
    27	    const r = deno(["run", "--stage", "smoke", "--protocol", protoPath, "--out", join(dir, "out")]);
    28	    expect(r.status, r.stderr).toBe(0);
    29	    const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, "out/meta.json"), "utf8"));
    30	    expect(meta.status).toBe("SMOKE");
    31	    expect(meta.statement).toMatch(/No model call/);
    32	    const v = deno(["validate", join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl")]);
    33	    expect(v.status, v.stdout + v.stderr).toBe(0);
    34	    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/fixedRule: \d+ lines, invalid 0, missing 0/);
    35	  }, 300_000);
    36	  it("validate fails on a corrupted line", () => {
    37	    const f = join(dir, "bad.jsonl");
    38	    const lines = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    39	    writeFileSync(f, [lines[0].replace('"abstain":false', '"abstain":true'), ...lines.slice(1)].join("\n") + "\n");
    40	    const v = deno(["validate", f, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    41	    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
    42	  }, 120_000);
    43	  it("refuses an unknown stage", () => {
    44	    const u = deno(["run", "--stage", "bogus", "--out", join(dir, "x")]);
    45	    expect(u.status).toBe(1);
    46	    expect(u.stderr).toMatch(/unknown --stage/);
    47	  }, 120_000);
    48	  it("validate fails on an empty predictions file, reporting every case as missing", () => {
    49	    const f = join(dir, "empty.jsonl");
    50	    writeFileSync(f, "");
    51	    const v = deno(["validate", f, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    52	    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
    53	    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/\(empty file\): 0 lines, invalid 0, missing [1-9]/);
    54	  }, 120_000);
    55	  it("validate fails on a stray case, and on a tampered envelope whose stale hash is left in place", () => {
    56	    const lines = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    57	    const mine = lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith('{"prediction":') && l.includes('"proposer":"fixedRule"'));
    58	    const stray = join(dir, "stray.jsonl");
    59	    writeFileSync(stray, [...mine, mine[0].replace(/"caseId":"c[0-9a-f]+"/, '"caseId":"cunknown"')].join("\n") + "\n");
    60	    const v = deno(["validate", stray, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    61	    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
    62	    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/extra 1/);
    63	    const envs = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    64	    const env0 = JSON.parse(envs[0]);
    65	    env0.census[0].mass += 1; // content changed, stored envelopeHash left stale
    66	    const tampered = join(dir, "tampered-envs.jsonl");
    67	    writeFileSync(tampered, [JSON.stringify(env0), ...envs.slice(1)].join("\n") + "\n");
    68	    const t = deno(["validate", join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "--envelopes", tampered]);
    69	    expect(t.status).toBe(1);
    70	    expect(t.stdout).toMatch(/unsound envelopes [1-9]/);
    71	  }, 120_000);
    72	  it("report keeps the generation source hash and records the renderer's separately", () => {
    73	    const metaPath = join(dir, "out/meta.json");
    74	    const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(metaPath, "utf8"));
    75	    writeFileSync(metaPath, JSON.stringify({ ...meta, sourceClosureSha256: "generated-by-older-code" }));
    76	    const r = deno(["report", "--out", join(dir, "out")]);
    77	    expect(r.status, r.stderr).toBe(0);
    78	    const after = JSON.parse(readFileSync(metaPath, "utf8"));
    79	    expect(after.sourceClosureSha256).toBe("generated-by-older-code");
    80	    expect(after.reportRenderedFromCanonicalData.sourceClosureSha256).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    81	  }, 120_000);
    82	  it("report re-renders the derived files byte for byte from the canonical data", () => {
    83	    const before = ["comparison.md", "report.html", "canonical.sha256"].map((n) => readFileSync(join(dir, "out", n), "utf8"));
    84	    const r = deno(["report", "--out", join(dir, "out")]);
    85	    expect(r.status, r.stderr).toBe(0);
    86	    const after = ["comparison.md", "report.html", "canonical.sha256"].map((n) => readFileSync(join(dir, "out", n), "utf8"));
    87	    expect(after).toEqual(before);
    88	  }, 120_000);
    89	  it("the confirm gate refuses an unfrozen protocol, a missing freeze file, a hash mismatch and an uncommitted freeze", () => {
    90	    const d = draftProtocol();
    91	    expect(confirmRefusal(d, null, undefined)).toMatch(/not frozen/);
    92	    const f = { ...d, frozen: true, minEffect: 50, devPrior: { up: 100, down: 100, none: 800 } };
    93	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, null, undefined)).toMatch(/missing/);
    94	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, "0".repeat(64), undefined)).toMatch(/does not match/);
    95	    expect(confirmRefusal({ ...f, minEffect: 51 }, protocolHash(f), undefined)).toMatch(/does not match/);
    96	    const h = protocolHash(f) + "\n", pj = JSON.stringify(f);
    97	    // A matching working tree is not enough: both files must be in HEAD, byte-equal.
    98	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, undefined)).toMatch(/committed/);
    99	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: null, freeze: null, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/protocol.json is not committed/);
   100	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj, freeze: null, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/freeze.sha256 is not committed/);
   101	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj + " ", freeze: h, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/differs from its committed/);
   102	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj, freeze: "x", protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toMatch(/differs from its committed/);
   103	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, h, { protocol: pj, freeze: h, protocolWorking: pj, freezeWorking: h })).toBeNull();
   104	  });
   105	});

     1	// The prediction-envelope harness CLI (docs/prediction-envelope-2026-11-01/PLAN.md). Deno, native CPU only: no GPU, no model call,
     2	// no network. Observer and tooling only; nothing here changes the dynamics.
     3	//
     4	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts protocol                       write the draft experiments/prediction-envelope/protocol.json (never overwrites)
     5	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage dev --out DIR      dev families only: DIR/dev/qualification.json (raw effects, tallies, stability)
     6	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts freeze --min-effect N [--out DIR]   set minEffect and devPrior from DIR/dev/qualification.json, write the freeze hash
     7	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage confirm --out DIR  all families; refuses unless protocol.json and protocol.freeze.sha256 are COMMITTED (HEAD) and match; `--exploratory yes` (last) skips the commit gate and marks the output EXPLORATORY
     8	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage smoke --protocol P --out DIR   a small protocol, no freeze check (output marked SMOKE in meta.json)
     9	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts validate FILE [--envelopes F]  check a predictions file against its envelopes (wrapped {proposer, prediction} or bare lines)
    10	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts report --out DIR               re-render report.html from the files in DIR
    11	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";

    13	import { cpus, platform, arch } from "node:os";
    14	import { dirname, join, relative, resolve } from "node:path";
    15	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    16	import { canonical, sha256Text } from "./lib/propose.ts";
    17	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./lib/prediction/envelope.ts";

    20	import { validatePredictionFile } from "./lib/prediction/validate.ts";
    21	import { sourceClosureHash as closureHash } from "./lib/prediction/closure.ts";

    23	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

    25	const EXP = join(root, "experiments/prediction-envelope");
    26	const PROTOCOL = join(EXP, "protocol.json");

    29	function args(argv: string[]): { cmd: string; pos: string[]; opt: Record<string, string> } {
    30	  const [cmd = "", ...rest] = argv;
    31	  const pos: string[] = [], opt: Record<string, string> = {};
    32	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
    33	    if (rest[i].startsWith("--")) {
    34	      opt[rest[i].slice(2)] = rest[i + 1] ?? "";
    35	      i++;
    36	    } else pos.push(rest[i]);
    37	  }
    38	  return { cmd, pos, opt };
    39	}
    40	const readProtocol = (path: string): Protocol => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Protocol;
    41	const writeText = (path: string, text: string): void => {
    42	  mkdirSync(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
    43	  writeFileSync(path, text);
    44	};
    45	function sh(cmd: string, a: string[]): string {
    46	  try {
    47	    return execFileSync(cmd, a, { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] }).trim();
    48	  } catch {
    49	    return "unknown";
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	const CLOSURE_ENTRIES = ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"];
    53	const sourceClosureHash = (): string => closureHash(root, CLOSURE_ENTRIES).sha256;
    54	/** The committed (HEAD) text of a repo path, or null when HEAD has no such file. */
    55	function committedText(path: string): string | null {
    56	  try {
    57	    return execFileSync("git", ["show", `HEAD:${relative(root, path)}`], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] });
    58	  } catch {
    59	    return null;
    60	  }
    61	}
    62	const readJsonl = <T>(path: string): T[] => readFileSync(path, "utf8").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as T);

    64	function main(): number {
    65	  const { cmd, pos, opt } = args(Deno.args);
    66	  if (cmd === "protocol") {
    67	    if (existsSync(PROTOCOL)) {
    68	      console.error(`${PROTOCOL} exists; not overwriting`);
    69	      return 1;
    70	    }
    71	    writeText(PROTOCOL, JSON.stringify(draftProtocol(), null, 2) + "\n");
    72	    console.log(`wrote ${PROTOCOL}`);
    73	    return 0;
    74	  }
    75	  if (cmd === "run") {
    76	    const stage = opt.stage ?? "";
    77	    const out = resolve(opt.out ?? "");
    78	    if (!opt.out) throw new Error("--out is required");
    79	    const protoPath = stage === "smoke" ? resolve(opt.protocol ?? "") : PROTOCOL;
    80	    const p = readProtocol(protoPath);
    81	    const t0 = Date.now();
    82	    if (stage === "dev") {
    83	      if (p.frozen) throw new Error("the protocol is already frozen; the dev stage is for qualification before the freeze");
    84	      const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    85	      const gen = generate(p, plan.dev, true);
    86	      writeText(join(out, "dev/qualification.json"), JSON.stringify(qualificationReport(p, gen), null, 1) + "\n");
    87	      console.log(`dev: ${gen.results.length} families, ${gen.variants.length} seed variants, ${Date.now() - t0} ms -> ${join(out, "dev/qualification.json")}`);
    88	      return 0;
    89	    }
    90	    if (stage === "confirm" || stage === "smoke") {
    91	      // `--exploratory` runs the confirm computation without the committed-freeze gate and marks every output EXPLORATORY: the test
    92	      // families are then not held out (their outcomes may already have been seen), so the run is a rehearsal, not a confirmation.
    93	      const exploratory = stage === "confirm" && opt.exploratory !== undefined;
    94	      if (stage === "confirm" && !exploratory) {
    95	        const freezeWorking = existsSync(FREEZE) ? readFileSync(FREEZE, "utf8") : null;
    96	        const refusal = confirmRefusal(p, freezeWorking, { protocol: committedText(PROTOCOL), freeze: committedText(FREEZE), protocolWorking: readFileSync(PROTOCOL, "utf8"), freezeWorking: freezeWorking ?? "" });
    97	        if (refusal) throw new Error(`refusing to confirm: ${refusal}`);
    98	      }
    99	      if (stage === "confirm" && exploratory) {
   100	        const refusal = freezeRefusal(p, existsSync(FREEZE) ? readFileSync(FREEZE, "utf8") : null);
   101	        if (refusal) throw new Error(`refusing even to rehearse: ${refusal}`);
   102	      }
   103	      const gen = generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true);
   104	      const built = buildOutputs(p, gen);
   105	      for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(built.files)) writeText(join(out, name), text);
   106	      const meta = {
   107	        stage,
   108	        status: stage === "smoke" ? "SMOKE" : exploratory ? "EXPLORATORY" : "CONFIRM",
   109	        ...(exploratory ? { exploratoryReason: "run before the freeze was committed, with test outcomes already inspected; not a held-out confirmation" } : {}),
   110	        gitSha: sh("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
   111	        gitDirty: sh("git", ["status", "--porcelain", "--", "tools", "packages", "experiments/prediction-envelope", "deno.json", "package.json", "tsconfig.json"]) !== "",
   112	        sourceClosureSha256: sourceClosureHash(),
   113	        versions: RUN_VERSIONS,
   114	        protocolSha256: protocolHash(p),
   115	        command: `deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts ${Deno.args.join(" ")}`,
   116	        hardware: { cpu: cpus()[0]?.model ?? "unknown", cores: cpus().length, platform: platform(), arch: arch() },
   117	        wallMs: Date.now() - t0,
   118	        cellSteps: built.comparison.validNonNullYieldAndCost.totalCellSteps,
   119	        statement: "CPU only (sim-ref). No model call, no GPU, no network, no paid compute.",
   120	      };
   121	      writeText(join(out, "meta.json"), JSON.stringify(meta, null, 2) + "\n");
   122	      console.log(`${stage}: ${gen.results.length} families, ${built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].split("\n").length - 1} cases, ${Date.now() - t0} ms -> ${out}`);
   123	      return 0;
   124	    }
   125	    throw new Error(`unknown --stage ${JSON.stringify(stage)} (dev, confirm, smoke)`);
   126	  }
   127	  if (cmd === "freeze") {
   128	    const out = resolve(opt.out ?? join(EXP, "v0"));
   129	    const min = Number(opt["min-effect"]);
   130	    if (!Number.isFinite(min) || min <= 0) throw new Error("--min-effect must be a positive number");
   131	    const p = readProtocol(PROTOCOL);
   132	    if (p.frozen) throw new Error("already frozen");
   133	    const q = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out, "dev/qualification.json"), "utf8")) as { protocolHash: string; devEffects: { bodyMass: number }[] };
   134	    if (q.protocolHash !== protocolHash(p)) throw new Error("dev/qualification.json was made with a different draft protocol");
   135	    p.minEffect = min;
   136	    p.devPrior = priorFromEffects(q.devEffects.map((e) => e.bodyMass), min);
   137	    p.frozen = true;
   138	    writeText(PROTOCOL, JSON.stringify(p, null, 2) + "\n");
   139	    writeText(FREEZE, protocolHash(p) + "\n");
   140	    console.log(`frozen: minEffect ${min}, devPrior ${JSON.stringify(p.devPrior)}, hash ${protocolHash(p)}`);
   141	    return 0;
   142	  }
   143	  if (cmd === "validate") {
   144	    const file = resolve(pos[0] ?? "");
   145	    const envPath = resolve(opt.envelopes ?? join(dirname(file), "envelopes.jsonl"));
   146	    const envelopes = readJsonl<PredictionEnvelope>(envPath);
   147	    const lines = readFileSync(file, "utf8").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "");
   148	    const groups = new Map<string, string[]>();
   149	    for (const l of lines) {
   150	      let proposer = "(bare)", body = l;
   151	      try {
   152	        const o = JSON.parse(l) as { proposer?: unknown; prediction?: unknown };
   153	        if (o && typeof o === "object" && typeof o.proposer === "string" && "prediction" in o) {
   154	          proposer = o.proposer;
   155	          body = JSON.stringify(o.prediction);
   156	        }
   157	      } catch {
   158	        /* the validator counts it */
   159	      }
   160	      (groups.get(proposer) ?? groups.set(proposer, []).get(proposer)!).push(body);
   161	    }
   162	    // The dev-only oracle is validated against dev envelopes only, when the protocol snapshot beside the file says which are dev.
   163	    const snap = join(dirname(file), "protocol.snapshot.json");
   164	    const devFamilies = existsSync(snap) ? new Set(splitPlanOf(readProtocol(snap)).dev) : null;
   165	    // An empty submission is still checked against the envelopes: every case is missing.
   166	    if (groups.size === 0) groups.set("(empty file)", []);
   167	    let bad = 0;
   168	    for (const [id, ls] of groups) {
   169	      const meant = devFamilies && ORACLE_IDS.includes(id) ? envelopes.filter((e) => devFamilies.has(e.familyId)) : envelopes;
   170	      const r = validatePredictionFile(ls, meant);
   171	      bad += r.invalid + r.missing.length + r.extra.length + r.duplicates.length + r.notObject + r.badEnvelopes.length;
   172	      console.log(`${id}: ${ls.length} lines, invalid ${r.invalid}, missing ${r.missing.length}${r.missing.length ? ` (${r.missing.slice(0, 5).join(", ")}${r.missing.length > 5 ? ", ..." : ""})` : ""}, extra ${r.extra.length}, duplicates ${r.duplicates.length}, unsound envelopes ${r.badEnvelopes.length}`);
   173	    }
   174	    return bad === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   175	  }
   176	  if (cmd === "report") {
   177	    // Re-renders comparison.md, report.html and canonical.sha256 from the canonical data files in DIR, and refreshes the source hash in meta.json.
   178	    if (!opt.out) throw new Error("--out is required");
   179	    const dir = resolve(opt.out);
   180	    const derived = rerender((name) => readFileSync(join(dir, name), "utf8"));
   181	    for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(derived)) writeText(join(dir, name), text);
   182	    const metaPath = join(dir, "meta.json");
   183	    if (existsSync(metaPath)) {
   184	      const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(metaPath, "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>;
   185	      // The generation hash stays as recorded (it names the code that made the outcomes); the renderer's hash is recorded separately.
   186	      meta.reportRenderedFromCanonicalData = { sourceClosureSha256: sourceClosureHash() };
   187	      writeText(metaPath, JSON.stringify(meta, null, 2) + "\n");
   188	    }
   189	    console.log(`report: re-rendered ${Object.keys(derived).join(", ")} in ${dir}`);
   190	    return 0;
   191	  }
   192	  console.error("usage: predict.ts protocol | run --stage dev|confirm|smoke --out DIR | freeze --min-effect N | validate FILE | report --out DIR");
   193	  return 2;
   194	}
   195	
   196	try {
   197	  Deno.exit(main());
   198	} catch (e) {
   199	  console.error((e as Error).message);
   200	  Deno.exit(1);

// The source-closure hash covers the transitive local imports of the harness, not a hand-picked list.
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { sourceClosure, sourceClosureHash } from "../lib/prediction/closure.ts";

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));

describe("sourceClosure", () => {
  const files = sourceClosure(root, ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"]);
  it("includes the executable dependencies the earlier hand-picked list missed", () => {
    for (const f of ["packages/schema/src/world.ts", "packages/schema/src/accounting.ts", "packages/schema/src/presets.ts", "packages/schema/src/genome.ts", "packages/schema/src/kernel.ts", "packages/sim-ref/src/step.ts", "packages/sim-ref/src/cells.ts", "tools/lib/report-html/page.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/closure.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts", "tools/lib/propose.ts"]) expect(files, f).toContain(f);
  });
  it("is sorted, repo-relative and free of node_modules", () => {
    expect([...files].sort()).toEqual(files);
    for (const f of files) {
      expect(f.startsWith("/")).toBe(false);
      expect(f).not.toContain("node_modules");
    }
  });
});

describe("sourceClosureHash", () => {
  const h = sourceClosureHash(root, ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"]);
  it("covers the resolver configuration and names the external specifiers", () => {
    expect(h.config).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["deno.json", "package.json"]));
    expect(h.externals).toContain("node:fs");
    expect(h.externals).toContain("fflate");
    expect(h.sha256).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  });
  it("is stable across calls", () => {
    expect(sourceClosureHash(root, ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"]).sha256).toBe(h.sha256);
  });
});

     1	// Orchestration for the prediction-envelope harness (PLAN.md, sections 7-9). Pure: no file or clock access, so every canonical
     2	// output is a deterministic function of the protocol. tools/predict.ts does the I/O. Proposers receive envelopes only; the scorer
     3	// alone reads outcomes.

    14	export const PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1;
    15	export interface Protocol extends FixtureProtocol {
    16	  protocolVersion: number;
    17	  readoutsVersion: number;
    18	  primaryReadout: "bodyMass";
    19	  /** In the primary readout's own unit; null until frozen. */
    20	  minEffect: number | null;
    21	  /** Candidate thresholds the dev qualification report tabulates. */
    22	  minEffectCandidates: number[];
    23	  salt: string;
    24	  families: FamilySpec[];
    25	  nDev: number;
    26	  nQualify: number;
    27	  /**
    28	   * Extra family cohorts, each split by its own hash ranking so the original cohort's dev/test/qualify assignment is unchanged by
    29	   * adding them: `nDev` of the cohort's families go to dev (none to the reserved qualify subset, so minEffect is never retuned on them).
    30	   */
    31	  cohorts?: { id: string; nDev: number }[];
    32	  donors: Record<string, string>;
    33	  proposers: ProposerParams;
    34	  devPrior: Probs | null;
    35	  bootstrap: { resamples: number; seed: number };
    36	  caps: { maxCellStepsPerFamily: number };
    37	  frozen: boolean;
    38	}
    39	
    40	export const protocolHash = (p: Protocol): string => sha256Text(canonical(p));
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * The confirm stage's gate. The protocol must be frozen, its hash must equal the recorded freeze hash, and BOTH files must be
    44	 * committed (HEAD blobs) byte-equal to the working-tree files: a working-tree-only freeze is not a freeze (PLAN.md, section 7).
    45	 * `committed` holds the HEAD blob texts (null when the file is not in HEAD). Returns the refusal, or null.
    46	 */
    47	export function freezeRefusal(p: Protocol, freezeText: string | null): string | null {
    48	  if (!p.frozen || p.minEffect === null || p.devPrior === null) return "protocol.json is not frozen (run the dev stage, then freeze)";
    49	  if (freezeText === null) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 is missing";
    50	  if (freezeText.trim() !== protocolHash(p)) return `protocol hash ${protocolHash(p)} does not match the frozen ${freezeText.trim()}`;
    51	  return null;
    52	}
    53	export interface CommittedCopies {
    54	  protocol: string | null;
    55	  freeze: string | null;
    56	  protocolWorking: string;
    57	  freezeWorking: string;
    58	}
    59	export function committedRefusal(c: CommittedCopies): string | null {
    60	  if (c.protocol === null) return "protocol.json is not committed (HEAD has no copy): commit the freeze before confirming";
    61	  if (c.freeze === null) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 is not committed (HEAD has no copy): commit the freeze before confirming";
    62	  if (c.protocol !== c.protocolWorking) return "protocol.json differs from its committed copy: commit the freeze before confirming";
    63	  if (c.freeze !== c.freezeWorking) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 differs from its committed copy: commit the freeze before confirming";
    64	  return null;
    65	}
    66	export const confirmRefusal = (p: Protocol, freezeText: string | null, committed: CommittedCopies | undefined): string | null =>
    67	  freezeRefusal(p, freezeText) ?? (committed ? committedRefusal(committed) : "no committed copy supplied: the freeze must be committed before test outcomes are generated");
    68	export function splitPlanOf(p: Protocol): SplitPlan {
    69	  const seen = new Set<string>();
    70	  for (const f of p.families) {
    71	    if (seen.has(f.id)) throw new Error(`duplicate family id ${f.id}`);
    72	    seen.add(f.id);
    73	  }
    74	  const base = assignSplits(p.families.filter((f) => f.cohort === undefined).map((f) => f.id), p.salt, p.nDev, p.nQualify);
    75	  const named = new Set((p.cohorts ?? []).map((c) => c.id));
    76	  for (const f of p.families) if (f.cohort !== undefined && !named.has(f.cohort)) throw new Error(`family ${f.id} names cohort ${f.cohort}, which protocol.cohorts does not declare`);
    77	  for (const c of p.cohorts ?? []) {
    78	    const ids = p.families.filter((f) => f.cohort === c.id).map((f) => f.id);
    79	    if (ids.length === 0) continue;
    80	    const sub = assignSplits(ids, `${p.salt}|cohort|${c.id}`, c.nDev, 0);
    81	    base.dev.push(...sub.dev);
    82	    base.test.push(...sub.test);
    83	  }
    84	  return base;
    85	}

    88	export function draftProtocol(): Protocol {
    89	  const lights = ["spots", "gradient"] as const;
    90	  const nutrients = [0, 1, 2];
    91	  const families: FamilySpec[] = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, k) => ({ id: `f${String(k).padStart(2, "0")}`, seed: 5000 + k, light: lights[k % 2], nutrient: nutrients[k % 3], founders: 3 + (k % 3) }));

    93	  // window), so a before/after metric and the numeric-trend baseline can be fooled by a trend the paired outcome removes. Founder counts
    94	  // were fixed before any outcome was generated; the probe only checked that such worlds rise (EVIDENCE.md, Amendment 2).

   139	export interface Generated {
   140	  results: FamilyResult[];
   141	  /** Seed-variant results for the qualify families (stability). */
   142	  variants: FamilyResult[];
   143	}
   144	
   145	export function generate(p: Protocol, familyIds: readonly string[], includeVariants: boolean): Generated {
   146	  const byId = new Map(p.families.map((f) => [f.id, f]));
   147	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   148	  const results: FamilyResult[] = [];
   149	  const variants: FamilyResult[] = [];
   150	  // Budget first: bounds and planned cell-steps are checked for every run (variants included) before anything is simulated.
   151	  const planned = plannedCellSteps(p);
   152	  if (planned > p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily) throw new Error(`a family run plans ${planned} cell-steps, over the cap ${p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily}`);
   153	  for (const id of familyIds) if (!byId.has(id)) throw new Error(`unknown family ${id}`);
   154	  for (const id of familyIds) {
   155	    const fam = byId.get(id)!;
   156	    results.push(runFamily(p, fam));
   157	    if (includeVariants && plan.qualify.includes(id)) variants.push(runFamily(p, fam, fam.seed + p.seedVariantOffset));
   158	  }
   159	  return { results, variants };
   160	}
   161	
   162	export interface CaseRow {
   163	  caseId: string;
   164	  familyId: string;
   165	  split: Split;
   166	  mechanismId: string;
   167	  expected: Direction3;
   168	  envelopeHash: string;
   169	  effect: { bodyMass: number; livingCells: number; freeNutrient: number };
   170	  before: number;
   171	  treated: number;
   172	  control: number;
   173	  treatedHash: string;
   174	}
   175	
   176	export function caseRows(p: Protocol, g: readonly FamilyResult[]): CaseRow[] {
   177	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   178	  const rows: CaseRow[] = [];
   179	  for (const r of g) {
   180	    const split: Split = plan.dev.includes(r.familyId) ? "dev" : "test";
   181	    for (const a of r.arms) {
   182	      const env = r.envelopes.find((e) => e.caseId === a.caseId)!;
   183	      const m = p.mechanisms.find((x) => x.id === a.mechanismId)!;
   184	      rows.push({
   185	        caseId: a.caseId,
   186	        familyId: r.familyId,
   187	        split,
   188	        mechanismId: a.mechanismId,
   189	        expected: m.expected,
   190	        envelopeHash: env.envelopeHash,
   191	        effect: { bodyMass: a.effect.bodyMass, livingCells: a.effect.livingCells, freeNutrient: a.effect.freeNutrient },
   192	        before: env.census[env.census.length - 1].mass,
   193	        treated: a.treated.bodyMass,
   194	        control: a.control.bodyMass,
   195	        treatedHash: a.treatedHash,
   196	      });
   197	    }
   198	  }
   199	  return rows;
   200	}
   201	
   202	/** Prespecified: a label that changes when the threshold is scaled by 3/4 or 5/4 is measurement-limited (kept in every denominator). */
   203	export function measurementLimited(effect: number, minEffect: number): boolean {
   204	  const l = labelOf(effect, minEffect);
   205	  return labelOf(effect, minEffect * 0.75) !== l || labelOf(effect, minEffect * 1.25) !== l;
   206	}
   207	
   208	/**
   209	 * The dev qualification report. Tuning (the minEffect candidates' tallies, cross-seed stability) uses ONLY the reserved `qualify`
   210	 * subset of dev families; `devEffects` carries every dev family's effects separately, for the dev class prior alone.
   211	 */
   212	export function qualificationReport(p: Protocol, dev: Generated) {
   213	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   214	  const allRows = caseRows(p, dev.results);
   215	  const rows = allRows.filter((r) => plan.qualify.includes(r.familyId));
   216	  const tallies = p.minEffectCandidates.map((t) => {
   217	    const perMech = p.mechanisms.map((m) => {
   218	      const rs = rows.filter((r) => r.mechanismId === m.id);
   219	      const count = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   220	      for (const r of rs) count[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, t)]++;
   221	      return { mechanism: m.id, expected: m.expected, ...count, agreeWithExpected: count[m.expected] };
   222	    });
   223	    return { minEffect: t, perMechanism: perMech };
   224	  });
   225	  const variantRows = caseRows(p, dev.variants);
   226	  const stability = p.minEffectCandidates.map((t) => {
   227	    const a: Direction3[] = [], b: Direction3[] = [];
   228	    for (const v of variantRows) {
   229	      const base = rows.find((r) => r.caseId === v.caseId);
   230	      if (!base) continue;
   231	      a.push(labelOf(base.effect.bodyMass, t));
   232	      b.push(labelOf(v.effect.bodyMass, t));
   233	    }
   234	    return { minEffect: t, ...signAgreement(a, b) };
   235	  });
   236	  return {
   237	    protocolHash: protocolHash(p),
   238	    note: "measured labels are never relabelled to match expectations; disagreements are reported. Tuning fields (effects, variantEffects, tallies, crossSeedEffectStability) cover the qualify subset only; devEffects (all dev families) feed the dev class prior only.",
   239	    qualifyFamilies: plan.qualify,
   240	    effects: rows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, familyId: r.familyId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass, livingCells: r.effect.livingCells, freeNutrient: r.effect.freeNutrient })),
   241	    devEffects: allRows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, familyId: r.familyId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass })),
   242	    variantEffects: variantRows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass })),
   243	    tallies,
   244	    crossSeedEffectStability: stability,
   245	    neutralityChecks: dev.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.neutralityChecks, 0),
   246	    cellSteps: dev.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0) + dev.variants.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0),
   247	  };
   248	}
   249	
   250	/** Class frequencies of a list of effects at a threshold, as per-mille summing to exactly 1000 (largest remainder; ties to the earlier of up, down, none). */
   251	export function priorFromEffects(effects: readonly number[], minEffect: number): Probs {
   252	  const c = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   253	  for (const e of effects) c[labelOf(e, minEffect)]++;
   254	  const n = effects.length;
   255	  if (n === 0) throw new Error("no dev effects");
   256	  const raw = CLASSES.map((k) => (c[k] * 1000) / n);
   257	  const fl = raw.map(Math.floor);
   258	  let left = 1000 - fl.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
   259	  const order = raw.map((r, i) => ({ i, f: r - fl[i] })).sort((a, b) => b.f - a.f || a.i - b.i);
   260	  for (const o of order) {
   261	    if (left <= 0) break;
   262	    fl[o.i]++;
   263	    left--;
   264	  }
   265	  return { up: fl[0], down: fl[1], none: fl[2] };
   266	}
   267	export const devPriorAt = (p: Protocol, dev: Generated, minEffect: number): Probs => priorFromEffects(caseRows(p, dev.results).map((r) => r.effect.bodyMass), minEffect);

   269	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   270	// Proposers and evaluation.
   271	
   272	export function buildProposers(p: Protocol, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): Proposer[] {
   273	  if (!p.devPrior) throw new Error("protocol has no devPrior: freeze it first");
   274	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   275	  const splitOf = (f: string): Split | undefined => (plan.dev.includes(f) ? "dev" : plan.test.includes(f) ? "test" : undefined);
   276	  const first = new Map<string, PredictionEnvelope>();
   277	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!first.has(e.familyId)) first.set(e.familyId, e);
   278	  return [
   279	    priorMajority(p.devPrior),
   280	    alwaysNone(),
     1	// Whole-family splits and the leak checks (PLAN.md, section 7). A family is one source history with all its windows and seed
     2	// variants; a split never cuts through one. Ranking is by SHA-256 of salt + family id, so the assignment is deterministic and
     3	// independent of input order. A reserved `qualify` subset of dev is used for all qualification and tuning.
     4	import { sha256Text } from "../propose.ts";
     5	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     6	
     7	export type Split = "dev" | "test";
     8	
     9	const rank = (salt: string, id: string): string => sha256Text(`${salt}|${id}`);
    10	
    11	export interface SplitPlan {
    12	  dev: string[];
    13	  test: string[];
    14	  qualify: string[];
    15	}
    16	
    17	/** First `nDev` families by hash rank are dev (the first `nQualify` of those are qualify), the rest test. Lists are in rank order. */
    18	export function assignSplits(familyIds: readonly string[], salt: string, nDev: number, nQualify: number): SplitPlan {
    19	  const uniq = [...new Set(familyIds)];
    20	  if (uniq.length !== familyIds.length) throw new Error("duplicate family ids");
    21	  if (nQualify > nDev || nDev >= uniq.length) throw new Error("need 0 <= nQualify <= nDev < number of families");
    22	  const ranked = uniq.map((id) => ({ id, h: rank(salt, id) })).sort((a, b) => (a.h < b.h ? -1 : a.h > b.h ? 1 : 0)).map((x) => x.id);
    23	  return { dev: ranked.slice(0, nDev), test: ranked.slice(nDev), qualify: ranked.slice(0, nQualify) };
    24	}
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * Donor assignment for the shuffled-evidence control: a derangement of the families within one split (a cyclic shift of the hash
    28	 * ranking, so no family is its own donor and none crosses a split), frozen into the protocol before any scoring.
    29	 */
    30	export function derangement(familyIds: readonly string[], salt: string): Record<string, string> {
    31	  if (familyIds.length < 2) throw new Error("a derangement needs at least two families");
    32	  const ranked = familyIds.map((id) => ({ id, h: rank(`${salt}|donor`, id) })).sort((a, b) => (a.h < b.h ? -1 : a.h > b.h ? 1 : 0)).map((x) => x.id);
    33	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    34	  ranked.forEach((id, i) => (out[id] = ranked[(i + 1) % ranked.length]));
    35	  return out;
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function donorsFor(plan: SplitPlan, salt: string): Record<string, string> {
    39	  return { ...derangement(plan.dev, salt), ...derangement(plan.test, salt) };
    40	}
    41	
    42	export interface DonorCheck {
    43	  ok: boolean;
    44	  reason?: string;
    45	}
    46	/** A donor envelope is acceptable only from a different family and history, in the same split. */
    47	export function donorAllowed(env: Pick<PredictionEnvelope, "familyId" | "historyId">, donor: Pick<PredictionEnvelope, "familyId" | "historyId">, splitOf: (familyId: string) => Split | undefined): DonorCheck {
    48	  if (donor.familyId === env.familyId) return { ok: false, reason: "donor is from the same family" };
    49	  if (donor.historyId === env.historyId) return { ok: false, reason: "donor is another window of the same history" };
    50	  const a = splitOf(env.familyId), b = splitOf(donor.familyId);
    51	  if (a === undefined || b === undefined || a !== b) return { ok: false, reason: "donor is from another split" };
    52	  return { ok: true };
    53	}
    54	
    55	/** Returns the problems found (empty when clean): overlapping families, a family in two splits, bad donors, test envelopes sharing a census with a dev one. */
    56	export function assertNoLeak(plan: SplitPlan, donors: Record<string, string>, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): string[] {
    57	  const errs: string[] = [];
    58	  const dev = new Set(plan.dev), test = new Set(plan.test);
    59	  for (const f of dev) if (test.has(f)) errs.push(`family ${f} is in both dev and test`);
    60	  for (const f of plan.qualify) if (!dev.has(f)) errs.push(`qualify family ${f} is not in dev`);
    61	  const splitOf = (f: string): Split | undefined => (dev.has(f) ? "dev" : test.has(f) ? "test" : undefined);
    62	  for (const [f, d] of Object.entries(donors)) {
    63	    if (f === d) errs.push(`family ${f} is its own donor`);
    64	    else if (splitOf(f) !== splitOf(d)) errs.push(`donor ${d} of ${f} is in another split`);
    65	  }
    66	  for (const f of [...dev, ...test]) if (donors[f] === undefined) errs.push(`family ${f} has no donor`);
    67	  const hist = new Map<string, string>();
    68	  for (const e of envelopes) {
    69	    if (splitOf(e.familyId) === undefined) errs.push(`envelope ${e.caseId} belongs to no split`);
    70	    const prev = hist.get(e.historyId);
    71	    if (prev !== undefined && prev !== e.familyId) errs.push(`history ${e.historyId} appears under two families`);
    72	    hist.set(e.historyId, e.familyId);
    73	  }
    74	  const devCensus = new Set<string>();
    75	  for (const e of envelopes) if (dev.has(e.familyId)) devCensus.add(JSON.stringify(e.census));
    76	  for (const e of envelopes) if (test.has(e.familyId) && devCensus.has(JSON.stringify(e.census))) errs.push(`test envelope ${e.caseId} carries a dev census`);
    77	  return errs;
    78	}

   272	export function buildProposers(p: Protocol, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): Proposer[] {
   273	  if (!p.devPrior) throw new Error("protocol has no devPrior: freeze it first");
   274	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   275	  const splitOf = (f: string): Split | undefined => (plan.dev.includes(f) ? "dev" : plan.test.includes(f) ? "test" : undefined);
   276	  const first = new Map<string, PredictionEnvelope>();
   277	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!first.has(e.familyId)) first.set(e.familyId, e);
   278	  return [
   279	    priorMajority(p.devPrior),
   280	    alwaysNone(),
   281	    fixedRule(p.proposers.fixedRule),
   282	    numericTrend(p.proposers.numericTrend),
   283	    v0Rules(p.proposers.v0Rules),
   284	    shuffledEvidence(p.proposers.numericTrend, p.donors, (f) => first.get(f), splitOf),
   285	    abstainAll(),
   286	    mechanismRule(p.proposers.mechanismRule),
   287	  ];
   288	}

   297	/** `eligible` decides which envelopes a proposer runs on (the dev-only oracle is never run on, serialised for, or shown on test cases). */
   298	export function runProposers(proposers: readonly Proposer[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], eligible: (pr: Proposer, env: PredictionEnvelope) => boolean = () => true): PredictionLine[] {
   299	  const out: PredictionLine[] = [];
   300	  for (const pr of proposers) for (const e of envelopes) if (eligible(pr, e)) out.push({ proposer: pr.id, prediction: pr.propose(structuredClone(e)) });
   301	  return out;
   302	}
   303	/** Proposer ids that are dev-only oracles (a file of theirs is validated against dev envelopes only). */
   304	export const ORACLE_IDS: readonly string[] = ["mechanismRule"];
   305	/** The standard eligibility: oracle proposers only on dev-split envelopes. */
   306	export const devOnlyOracle = (p: Protocol) => {
   307	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   308	  return (pr: Proposer, env: PredictionEnvelope): boolean => pr.role !== "oracle" || plan.dev.includes(env.familyId);
   309	};
   310	
   311	export function scoredCases(lines: readonly PredictionLine[], proposerId: string, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], rows: readonly CaseRow[], minEffect: number): ScoredCase[] {
   312	  const mine = lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === proposerId).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction));
   313	  const check = validatePredictionFile(mine, envelopes);
   314	  const byRow = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.caseId, r]));
   315	  const out: ScoredCase[] = [];
   316	  for (const e of envelopes) {
   317	    const row = byRow.get(e.caseId);
   318	    if (!row) continue;
   319	    const got = check.byCase.get(e.caseId);
   320	    const prediction = got?.prediction ?? null;
   321	    const status: ScoredCase["status"] = !got ? "missing" : !prediction ? "invalid" : prediction.abstain ? "abstained" : "answered";
   322	    out.push({ caseId: e.caseId, familyId: e.familyId, outcome: labelOf(row.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), prediction: status === "answered" ? prediction : null, status });
   323	  }
   324	  return out;
   325	}

   327	export interface TableRow extends ProposerScore {
   328	  role: string;
   329	  brierAtMatchedCoverage: number | null;
   330	  accuracyAtMatchedCoverage: number | null;

   332	export interface SplitTable {
   333	  split: string;
   334	  nCases: number;
   335	  nFamilies: number;
   336	  classCounts: Record<Direction3, number>;
   337	  meanAbsEffect: Record<string, number | null>;
   338	  rows: TableRow[];
   339	}
   340	
   341	export function tableFor(p: Protocol, name: string, proposers: readonly Proposer[], lines: readonly PredictionLine[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], rows: readonly CaseRow[], pick: (r: CaseRow) => boolean): SplitTable {
   342	  const sel = rows.filter(pick);
   343	  const ids = new Set(sel.map((r) => r.caseId));
   344	  const envs = envelopes.filter((e) => ids.has(e.caseId));
   345	  const prior = p.devPrior!;
   346	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
   347	  const byP: Record<string, ScoredCase[]> = {};
   348	  for (const pr of proposers) byP[pr.id] = scoredCases(lines, pr.id, envs, sel, minEffect);
   349	  // The coverage floor (a proposer that answers nothing) is left out of the matched set, else it would empty it.
   350	  const answering = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(byP).filter(([, cs]) => cs.some((c) => c.status === "answered")));
   351	  const matched = matchedCoverageSet(answering);
   352	  const classCounts: Record<Direction3, number> = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   353	  for (const r of sel) classCounts[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)]++;
   354	  const mean = (f: (r: CaseRow) => number): number | null => (sel.length ? sel.reduce((s, r) => s + Math.abs(f(r)), 0) / sel.length : null);
   355	  return {
   356	    split: name,
   357	    nCases: sel.length,
   358	    nFamilies: new Set(sel.map((r) => r.familyId)).size,
   359	    classCounts,
   360	    meanAbsEffect: { [`bodyMass (${READOUT_UNITS.bodyMass})`]: mean((r) => r.effect.bodyMass), [`livingCells (${READOUT_UNITS.livingCells}, diagnostic)`]: mean((r) => r.effect.livingCells), [`freeNutrient (${READOUT_UNITS.freeNutrient}, diagnostic)`]: mean((r) => r.effect.freeNutrient) },
   361	    rows: proposers.map((pr) => {
   362	      const s = scoreProposer(pr.id, byP[pr.id], prior);
   363	      const m = matched.size ? scoreProposer(pr.id, byP[pr.id], prior, matched) : null;
   364	      return { ...s, role: pr.role, brierAtMatchedCoverage: m?.brierAnswered ?? null, accuracyAtMatchedCoverage: m?.directionalAccuracy ?? null };
   365	    }),
   366	  };
   367	}
