     1	// Static notebook view (PLAN.md, section 10): per case an observation block (census sparkline, frame thumbnails), a hypothesis
     2	// block (probabilities per proposer, with the free-text reason, escaped) and a measured-outcome block (the paired result), visibly
     3	// distinct. Failed predictions first; invalid or missing ones listed in their own section. No scripts, no external URLs, works from
     4	// file://. A pure function of its inputs.
     5	import { escapeHtml } from "../report-html/page.ts";
     6	import { labelOf } from "./fixtures.ts";
     7	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     8	import type { CaseRow, Comparison, PredictionLine, SplitTable } from "./run.ts";
     9	import { argmax, validatePredictionFile } from "./validate.ts";
    10	
    11	const esc = escapeHtml;
    12	const pct = (v: number | null): string => (v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(3));
    13	
    14	function spark(env: PredictionEnvelope): string {
    15	  const pts = env.census.map((c) => c.mass);
    16	  const lo = Math.min(...pts), hi = Math.max(...pts), span = Math.max(1, hi - lo);
    17	  const w = 160, h = 40;
    18	  const d = pts.map((v, i) => `${((i / Math.max(1, pts.length - 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h - 2 - ((v - lo) / span) * (h - 4)).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
    19	  return `<svg class="spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="body mass over the observed steps ${env.census[0].step} to ${env.census[env.census.length - 1].step}"><polyline points="${d}" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg>`;
    20	}
    21	/** The last frame as four-level quantised paths (one path per level, empty blocks omitted), to keep the page small. */
    22	function thumb(f: PredictionEnvelope["frames"][number]): string {
    23	  const max = Math.max(1, ...f.mass), s = 6;
    24	  const paths: string[] = ["", "", "", ""];
    25	  f.mass.forEach((m, i) => {
    26	    if (m <= 0) return;
    27	    const lvl = Math.min(3, Math.floor((m / max) * 4));
    28	    paths[lvl] += `M${(i % f.side) * s} ${Math.floor(i / f.side) * s}h${s}v${s}h-${s}z`;
    29	  });
    30	  const body = paths.map((d, i) => (d ? `<path d="${d}" fill="currentColor" fill-opacity="${[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1][i]}"/>` : "")).join("");
    31	  return `<figure class="thumb"><svg viewBox="0 0 ${f.side * s} ${f.side * s}" role="img" aria-label="mass by block at step ${f.step}">${body}</svg><figcaption>t=${f.step}</figcaption></figure>`;
    32	}
    33	function bar(p: { up: number; down: number; none: number }): string {
    34	  return `<span class="bar" aria-hidden="true"><i class="up" style="width:${p.up / 10}%"></i><i class="down" style="width:${p.down / 10}%"></i><i class="none" style="width:${p.none / 10}%"></i></span>`;
    35	}
    36	
    37	function tableHtml(t: SplitTable): string {
    38	  const head = ["proposer", "role", "n", "answered", "abstained", "invalid", "missing", "coverage", "Brier (imputed)", "Brier (answered)", "direction acc.", "balanced acc.", "acc. @ matched coverage", "valid non-null yield"];
    39	  const body = t.rows.map((r) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(r.id)}</th><td>${esc(r.role)}</td><td>${r.n}</td><td>${r.answered}</td><td>${r.abstained}</td><td>${r.invalid}</td><td>${r.missing}</td><td>${pct(r.coverage)}</td><td>${pct(r.brierImputed)}</td><td>${pct(r.brierAnswered)}</td><td>${pct(r.directionalAccuracy)}</td><td>${pct(r.balancedAccuracy)}</td><td>${pct(r.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage)}</td><td>${pct(r.validNonNullYield)}</td></tr>`).join("");
    40	  return `<h3>${esc(t.split)} (${t.nCases} cases, ${t.nFamilies} families; up ${t.classCounts.up}, down ${t.classCounts.down}, none ${t.classCounts.none})</h3><div class="scroll"><table><thead><tr>${head.map((h) => `<th scope="col">${esc(h)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table></div>`;
    41	}

    43	export interface ReportInput {
    44	  comparison: Comparison;
    45	  envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[];
    46	  lines: readonly PredictionLine[];
    47	  rows: readonly CaseRow[];
    48	  minEffect: number;
    49	  proposerIds: readonly string[];
    50	  /** Whether a proposer was run on an envelope (the dev-only oracle is not shown, and not counted missing, on test cases). Default: always. */
    51	  eligible?: (proposerId: string, env: PredictionEnvelope) => boolean;
    52	}
    53	
    54	export function renderReport(inp: ReportInput): string {
    55	  const { comparison: c, envelopes, lines, rows, minEffect } = inp;
    56	  const eligible = inp.eligible ?? (() => true);
    57	  const proposerIds = inp.proposerIds;
    58	  const idsFor = (e: PredictionEnvelope): string[] => proposerIds.filter((id) => eligible(id, e));
    59	  const rowOf = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.caseId, r]));
    60	  // Per-proposer validation (so invalid lines show up here as they do in the scorer).
    61	  const status = new Map<string, Map<string, "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing">>();
    62	  const preds = new Map<string, Map<string, PredictionLine["prediction"] | null>>();
    63	  for (const id of proposerIds) {
    64	    const chk = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === id).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), envelopes);
    65	    const s = new Map<string, "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing">(), pm = new Map<string, PredictionLine["prediction"] | null>();
    66	    for (const e of envelopes) {
    67	      const g = chk.byCase.get(e.caseId);
    68	      s.set(e.caseId, !g ? "missing" : !g.prediction ? "invalid" : g.prediction.abstain ? "abstained" : "answered");
    69	      pm.set(e.caseId, g?.prediction ?? null);
    70	    }
    71	    status.set(id, s);
    72	    preds.set(id, pm);
    73	  }
    74	  const envOf = new Map(envelopes.map((e) => [e.caseId, e]));
    75	  const failures = (caseId: string): number => {
    76	    const r = rowOf.get(caseId)!;
    77	    const o = labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
    78	    let k = 0;
    79	    for (const id of idsFor(envOf.get(caseId)!)) {
    80	      const st = status.get(id)!.get(caseId)!;
    81	      const p = preds.get(id)!.get(caseId);
    82	      if (st === "invalid" || st === "missing" || (st === "answered" && p?.probs && argmax(p.probs) !== o)) k++;
    83	    }
    84	    return k;
    85	  };
    86	  const ordered = [...envelopes].filter((e) => rowOf.has(e.caseId)).sort((a, b) => failures(b.caseId) - failures(a.caseId) || (a.caseId < b.caseId ? -1 : 1));
    87	  const caseHtml = (e: PredictionEnvelope): string => {
    88	    const r = rowOf.get(e.caseId)!;
    89	    const o = labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
    90	    const hyp = idsFor(e)
    91	      .map((id) => {
    92	        const st = status.get(id)!.get(e.caseId)!;
    93	        const p = preds.get(id)!.get(e.caseId);
    94	        if (!p || st === "invalid" || st === "missing") return `<li class="bad"><b>${esc(id)}</b> ${esc(st)}</li>`;
    95	        if (p.abstain) return `<li><b>${esc(id)}</b> abstains <q>${esc(p.reason ?? "")}</q></li>`;
    96	        const a = argmax(p.probs!);
    97	        return `<li class="${a === o ? "ok" : "bad"}"><b>${esc(id)}</b> ${bar(p.probs!)} up ${p.probs!.up} / down ${p.probs!.down} / none ${p.probs!.none} per-mille, says ${esc(a)} <q>${esc(p.reason ?? "")}</q></li>`;
    98	      })
    99	      .join("");
   100	    const op = e.task.op;
   101	    const opText = op.kind === "none" ? "no operation (sham)" : `${op.kind} at (${op.x}, ${op.y}) radius ${op.r}${op.amount !== undefined ? `, amount ${op.amount}` : ""}`;
   102	    return `<article id="${esc(e.caseId)}" class="case"><h3>${esc(e.caseId)} <small>family ${esc(e.familyId)}, ${esc(r.split)}</small></h3>
   103	<section class="obs"><h4>Observation (steps up to ${e.task.forkStep})</h4>${spark(e)}<div class="thumbs">${thumb(e.frames[e.frames.length - 1])}</div></section>
   104	<section class="hyp"><h4>Hypotheses</h4><p>Question: ${esc(opText)} at step ${e.task.forkStep + e.task.delta}; direction of the ${esc(e.task.readout)} difference at step ${e.task.forkStep + e.task.horizon}.</p><ul>${hyp}</ul></section>
   105	<section class="out"><h4>Measured outcome</h4><p>treated ${r.treated}, control ${r.control}, effect ${r.effect.bodyMass} (minimum effect ${minEffect}) so <b>${esc(o)}</b>; fixture expectation ${esc(r.expected)}${o === r.expected ? "" : " (measured differs)"}.</p></section></article>`;
   106	  };
   107	  const bad = ordered.filter((e) => idsFor(e).some((id) => ["invalid", "missing"].includes(status.get(id)!.get(e.caseId)!)));
   108	  const css = `body{font:15px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:72rem;margin:0 auto;padding:1rem;color:#1d2328;background:#fbfaf6}table{border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px}th,td{border:1px solid #cfc9bb;padding:.25rem .5rem;text-align:left}.scroll{overflow-x:auto}.case{border:1px solid #cfc9bb;border-radius:6px;margin:1rem 0;padding:.5rem 1rem;display:grid;gap:.5rem}.obs{background:#eef3f6;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px;color:#27475a}.hyp{background:#fbf3e3;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px}.out{background:#e8f2e8;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px}.spark{width:10rem;height:2.5rem}.thumbs{display:flex;gap:.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap}.thumb{margin:0}.thumb svg{width:3.5rem;height:3.5rem;background:#fff;border:1px solid #cfc9bb}.thumb figcaption{font-size:11px}ul{padding-left:1rem;margin:.25rem 0}li.ok b{color:#1f6b2a}li.bad b{color:#9b2c1c}q{color:#555}.bar{display:inline-flex;width:6rem;height:.6rem;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:.4rem;background:#ddd}.bar i{display:block;height:100%}.bar .up{background:#2a7a3a}.bar .down{background:#b23b2a}.bar .none{background:#8a8f94}small{color:#666;font-weight:normal}code{overflow-wrap:anywhere}`;
   109	  return `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Prediction envelope v0: notebook</title><style>${css}</style></head><body>
   110	<h1>Prediction envelope v0: notebook</h1>
   111	<p>${esc(c.noModelStatement)}</p>
   112	<p>Protocol hash <code>${esc(c.protocolHash)}</code>. Strongest simple baseline on dev: <b>${esc(c.strongestBaselineOnDev)}</b>. Primary readout: body mass (B+P), minimum effect ${minEffect}. Failed predictions first.</p>
   113	<p>The mechanism oracle is a dev diagnostic: it is run on, and shown for, dev cases only.</p>
   114	<h2>Comparison</h2>${tableHtml(c.dev)}${tableHtml(c.test)}${tableHtml(c.testExcludingMeasurementLimited)}
   115	<h2>Invalid or missing predictions</h2>${bad.length ? `<ul>${bad.map((e) => `<li><a href="#${esc(e.caseId)}">${esc(e.caseId)}</a></li>`).join("")}</ul>` : "<p>None.</p>"}
   116	<h2>Cases</h2>${ordered.map(caseHtml).join("\n")}
   117	</body></html>
   118	`;
   119	}
     1	// Splits and leak checks (PLAN.md, section 7).
     2	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3	import { assertNoLeak, assignSplits, derangement, donorAllowed, donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
     4	import { FEED, censusSeries, synthEnv } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";
     5	
     6	const ids = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `f${i}`);
     7	
     8	describe("splits", () => {
     9	  it("never lets a family straddle dev and test, and qualify lies inside dev", () => {
    10	    const p = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    11	    expect(p.dev).toHaveLength(6);
    12	    expect(p.test).toHaveLength(6);
    13	    expect(p.dev.filter((f) => p.test.includes(f))).toEqual([]);
    14	    expect(p.qualify.every((f) => p.dev.includes(f))).toBe(true);
    15	    expect([...p.dev, ...p.test].sort()).toEqual([...ids].sort());
    16	  });
    17	  it("is deterministic under input permutation and depends on the salt", () => {
    18	    const a = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    19	    expect(assignSplits([...ids].reverse(), "salt", 6, 2)).toEqual(a);
    20	    expect(assignSplits(ids, "other", 6, 2)).not.toEqual(a);
    21	  });
    22	  it("a derangement has no fixed point and stays inside its split", () => {
    23	    const p = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    24	    const d = donorsFor(p, "salt");
    25	    for (const f of p.dev) expect(p.dev).toContain(d[f]);
    26	    for (const f of p.test) expect(p.test).toContain(d[f]);
    27	    for (const f of ids) expect(d[f]).not.toBe(f);
    28	    expect(new Set(Object.values(d)).size).toBe(ids.length);
    29	    expect(derangement(["a", "b"], "s")).toEqual({ a: "b", b: "a" });
    30	    expect(() => derangement(["a"], "s")).toThrow();
    31	  });
    32	  it("donorAllowed refuses the same family, the same history and another split", () => {
    33	    const a = { familyId: "fA", historyId: "fA:s1" };
    34	    const sp = (f: string) => (f === "fX" ? "test" : "dev") as "dev" | "test";
    35	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fA", historyId: "fA:s2" }, sp).ok).toBe(false);
    36	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fB", historyId: "fA:s1" }, sp).ok).toBe(false);
    37	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fX", historyId: "fX:s1" }, sp).ok).toBe(false);
    38	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fB", historyId: "fB:s1" }, sp).ok).toBe(true);
    39	  });
    40	});
    41	
    42	describe("assertNoLeak", () => {
    43	  const p = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    44	  const d = donorsFor(p, "salt");
    45	  const envs = ids.map((f, i) => synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: f, caseId: `c${f}`, census: censusSeries(1000 + i, 1) }));
    46	  it("is clean on a sound plan", () => {
    47	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, d, envs)).toEqual([]);
    48	  });
    49	  it("fails on an injected shared family", () => {
    50	    expect(assertNoLeak({ ...p, test: [...p.test, p.dev[0]] }, d, envs).join()).toMatch(/both dev and test/);
    51	  });
    52	  it("fails on a bad donor (self or cross-split)", () => {
    53	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, { ...d, [p.dev[0]]: p.dev[0] }, envs).join()).toMatch(/own donor/);
    54	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, { ...d, [p.dev[0]]: p.test[0] }, envs).join()).toMatch(/another split/);
    55	  });
    56	  it("fails when a test envelope carries a dev census, or a history sits under two families", () => {
    57	    const dup = envs.map((e) => (e.familyId === p.test[0] ? { ...e, census: envs.find((x) => x.familyId === p.dev[0])!.census } : e));
    58	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, d, dup).join()).toMatch(/dev census/);
    59	    const hist = envs.map((e) => (e.familyId === p.test[0] ? { ...e, historyId: envs[0].historyId } : e));
    60	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, d, hist).join()).toMatch(/two families/);
    61	  });
    62	});
    63	
    64	describe("cohort splits (draft protocol)", () => {
    65	  it("adding the rising cohort leaves the original cohort's split untouched and splits the cohort whole-family", async () => {
    66	    const { draftProtocol, splitPlanOf } = await import("../lib/prediction/run.ts");
    67	    const full = draftProtocol();
    68	    const orig = { ...full, cohorts: undefined, families: full.families.filter((f) => f.cohort === undefined) };
    69	    const a = splitPlanOf(orig), b = splitPlanOf(full);
    70	    const origIds = new Set(orig.families.map((f) => f.id));
    71	    expect(b.dev.filter((f) => origIds.has(f))).toEqual(a.dev);
    72	    expect(b.test.filter((f) => origIds.has(f))).toEqual(a.test);
    73	    expect(b.qualify).toEqual(a.qualify);
    74	    const rising = full.families.filter((f) => f.cohort === "rising").map((f) => f.id);
    75	    expect(rising).toHaveLength(8);
    76	    expect(b.dev.filter((f) => rising.includes(f))).toHaveLength(4);
    77	    expect(b.test.filter((f) => rising.includes(f))).toHaveLength(4);
    78	    expect(b.qualify.some((f) => rising.includes(f))).toBe(false);
    79	    expect([...b.dev, ...b.test].sort()).toEqual(full.families.map((f) => f.id).sort());
    80	  });
    81	  it("a family id repeated across cohorts is refused", async () => {
    82	    const { draftProtocol, splitPlanOf } = await import("../lib/prediction/run.ts");
    83	    const p = draftProtocol();
    84	    p.families.find((f) => f.cohort === "rising")!.id = "f00";
    85	    expect(() => splitPlanOf(p)).toThrow(/duplicate family id f00/);
    86	  });
    87	  it("a family naming an undeclared cohort is refused", async () => {
    88	    const { draftProtocol, splitPlanOf } = await import("../lib/prediction/run.ts");
    89	    const p = draftProtocol();
    90	    p.cohorts = [];
    91	    expect(() => splitPlanOf(p)).toThrow(/cohort/);
    92	  });
    93	});
     1	// The static notebook view, the outputs' byte reproducibility, and the comparison's shape.
     2	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";

    12	const T = 240_000;

    14	describe("report.html", () => {
    15	  const { p, built } = tinyPipeline();
    16	  const html = built.files["report.html"];
    17	  const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    18	  it("contains every case id, no scripts and no external URLs", () => {
    19	    for (const e of envs) expect(html).toContain(`id="${e.caseId}"`);
    20	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/<script/i);
    21	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/https?:\/\//i);
    22	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/\son\w+=/i);
    23	  }, T);
    24	  it("shows failed predictions first and escapes the free-text reason", () => {
    25	    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    26	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    27	    const evil = lines.map((l: { proposer: string; prediction: { reason: string } }) => (l.proposer === "fixedRule" ? { ...l, prediction: { ...l.prediction, reason: "<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>" } } : l));
    28	    const out = renderReport({ comparison: built.comparison, envelopes: envs, lines: evil as never, rows, minEffect: p.minEffect!, proposerIds: ["fixedRule", "numericTrend"] });
    29	    expect(out).not.toContain("<img src=x");
    30	    expect(out).toContain("&lt;img src=x");
    31	    const first = out.match(/<article[\s\S]*?<\/article>/)![0];
    32	    expect(first).toContain('<li class="bad">');
    33	    const lastArticle = [...out.matchAll(/<article[\s\S]*?<\/article>/g)].pop()![0];
    34	    const bads = (h: string) => (h.match(/<li class="bad">/g) ?? []).length;
    35	    expect(bads(first)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(bads(lastArticle));
    36	  }, T);
    37	  it("lists invalid predictions in their own section", () => {
    38	    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    39	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    40	    const broken = lines.map((l: { proposer: string; prediction: Record<string, unknown> }, i: number) => (l.proposer === "fixedRule" && i % 5 === 0 ? { ...l, prediction: { ...l.prediction, probs: { up: 1, down: 1, none: 1 } } } : l));
    41	    const out = renderReport({ comparison: built.comparison, envelopes: envs, lines: broken as never, rows, minEffect: p.minEffect!, proposerIds: ["fixedRule"] });
    42	    expect(out).toMatch(/Invalid or missing predictions<\/h2><ul>/);
    43	  }, T);
    44	});
    45	
    46	describe("outputs", () => {
    47	  const { p, built } = tinyPipeline();
    48	  it("are byte-reproducible: a second generation gives identical canonical files", () => {
    49	    const again = buildOutputs(p, generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true));
    50	    for (const n of CANONICAL_FILES) expect(again.files[n], n).toBe(built.files[n]);
    51	    expect(again.canonicalSha256).toBe(built.canonicalSha256);
    52	  }, T);
    53	  it("canonical.sha256 lists exactly the canonical files with their hashes", () => {
    54	    const listed = built.canonicalSha256.trim().split("\n").map((l) => l.split("  "));
    55	    expect(listed.map((x) => x[1])).toEqual([...CANONICAL_FILES]);
    56	    for (const [h, n] of listed) expect(h).toBe(sha256Text(built.files[n]));
    57	  });
    58	  it("every proposer's file validates against the envelopes; shuffled never uses its own family; the oracle is absent from the test table", () => {
    59	    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    60	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    61	    const ids = new Set(lines.map((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer));
    62	    const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    63	    for (const id of ids) {
    64	      const meant = id === "mechanismRule" ? envs.filter((e: { familyId: string }) => plan.dev.includes(e.familyId)) : envs;
    65	      const r = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer === id).map((l: { prediction: unknown }) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), meant);
    66	      expect(r.invalid + r.missing.length + r.extra.length, id).toBe(0);
    67	    }
    68	    expect(built.comparison.test.rows.map((r) => r.id)).not.toContain("mechanismRule");
    69	    expect(built.comparison.dev.rows.map((r) => r.id)).toContain("mechanismRule");
    70	    expect(built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!.coverage).toBe(0);
    71	    expect(built.comparison.perFamily.test.intervals).toBe(false);
    72	  });
    73	  it("the dev-only oracle is run on, serialised for and shown on dev cases only", () => {
    74	    const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    75	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as { proposer: string; prediction: { caseId: string } });
    76	    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as { caseId: string; familyId: string });
    77	    const fam = new Map(envs.map((e) => [e.caseId, e.familyId]));
    78	    const oracle = lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === "mechanismRule");
    79	    expect(oracle.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    80	    for (const l of oracle) expect(plan.dev, l.prediction.caseId).toContain(fam.get(l.prediction.caseId));
    81	    const html = built.files["report.html"];
    82	    for (const e of envs.filter((x) => plan.test.includes(x.familyId))) {
    83	      const art = html.match(new RegExp(`<article id="${e.caseId}"[\\s\\S]*?</article>`))![0];
    84	      expect(art).not.toContain("mechanismRule");
    85	    }
    86	    expect(devOnlyOracle(p)({ id: "mechanismRule", role: "oracle", propose: () => null as never }, { familyId: plan.test[0] } as never)).toBe(false);
    87	    expect(built.comparison.fileDiagnostics.mechanismRule.missing).toBe(0);
    88	  });
    89	  it("file-level diagnostics (extra, duplicate, invalid) reach the comparison, not just the per-case scores", () => {
    90	    expect(Object.keys(built.comparison.fileDiagnostics)).toContain("fixedRule");
    91	    expect(built.files["comparison.md"]).toContain("File-level diagnostics");
    92	    expect(built.comparison.fileDiagnostics.fixedRule).toMatchObject({ invalid: 0, missing: 0, extra: 0, duplicates: 0, notObject: 0, badEnvelopes: 0 });
    93	  });
    94	  it("tableFor keeps the matched-coverage set non-empty when abstainAll is present (zero coverage is excluded from it)", () => {
    95	    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    96	    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    97	    const ps = [fixedRule(p.proposers.fixedRule), alwaysNone(), abstainAll()];
    98	    const lines = ps.flatMap((pr) => envs.map((e: PredictionEnvelope) => ({ proposer: pr.id, prediction: pr.propose(e) })));
    99	    const t = tableFor(p, "t", ps, lines, envs, rows, () => true);
   100	    for (const id of ["fixedRule", "alwaysNone"]) expect(t.rows.find((r) => r.id === id)!.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage, id).not.toBeNull();
   101	    expect(t.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!.coverage).toBe(0);
   102	  });
   103	  it("a file with an extra unknown case and a duplicate is reported by validatePredictionFile, and the comparison would carry it", () => {
   104	    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
   105	    const mine = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l)).filter((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer === "alwaysNone").map((l: { prediction: unknown }) => l.prediction as Record<string, unknown>);
   106	    const lines = [...mine, { ...mine[0], caseId: "cunknown" }, mine[1]].map((x) => JSON.stringify(x));

   180	describe("trendConfoundOf on a synthetic rising set", () => {
   181	  const row = (mechanismId: string, before: number, treated: number, control: number): CaseRow => ({ caseId: mechanismId, familyId: "f", split: "test", mechanismId, expected: "none", envelopeHash: "h", effect: { bodyMass: treated - control, livingCells: 0, freeNutrient: 0 }, before, treated, control, treatedHash: "t" });
   182	  const rows = [row("sham", 100, 160, 160), row("lowDoseFeed", 100, 163, 160), row("feedOccupied", 100, 260, 160), row("lesionOccupied", 100, 40, 160)];
   183	  it("the naive rule is fooled by the rising world where the paired rule is not", () => {
   184	    const t = trendConfoundOf(rows, 50);
   185	    expect(t.sham).toEqual({ n: 1, naiveUp: 1, pairedNone: 1 });
   186	    expect(t.lowDose).toEqual({ n: 1, naiveUp: 1, pairedNone: 1 });
   187	    expect(t.pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy).toBe(0.25);
   188	    expect(t.naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy).toBe(0.75);
   189	    expect(t.risingCases).toBe(3);
   190	    expect(t.totalCases).toBe(4);
   191	  });
   192	  it("on a declining set the confound cannot show: no rising cases, naive never says up", () => {
   193	    const t = trendConfoundOf([row("sham", 200, 150, 150), row("lowDoseFeed", 200, 153, 150)], 50);
   194	    expect(t.risingCases).toBe(0);
   195	    expect(t.sham.naiveUp).toBe(0);
   196	  });
   197	});
   198	
   199	describe("trend-confound truth table", () => {
   200	  // Hand-specified: a rising world (before = 100). Paired effect of sham and a low-dose feed is 0 (none); the naive before/after
   201	  // rule sees treated 160 - before 100 = +60 and calls both "up" at a minimum effect of 50.
   202	  const rows = [
   203	    { name: "sham", before: 100, treated: 160, control: 160, pairedLabel: "none", naiveLabel: "up" },
   204	    { name: "lowDose", before: 100, treated: 163, control: 160, pairedLabel: "none", naiveLabel: "up" },
   205	    { name: "feed", before: 100, treated: 260, control: 160, pairedLabel: "up", naiveLabel: "up" },
   206	    { name: "lesion", before: 100, treated: 40, control: 160, pairedLabel: "down", naiveLabel: "down" },
   207	  ] as const;
   208	  it("matches the hand-derived table", () => {
   209	    for (const r of rows) {
   210	      expect(labelOf(r.treated - r.control, 50), r.name).toBe(r.pairedLabel);
   211	      expect(labelOf(r.treated - r.before, 50), r.name).toBe(r.naiveLabel);
   212	    }
   213	    // alwaysRising ("up" everywhere): right on 2 of 4 by the paired rule, 3 of 4 by the naive rule.
   214	    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.pairedLabel === "up").length).toBe(1);
   215	    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.naiveLabel === "up").length).toBe(3);
   216	  });
   217	});
   218	
   219	describe("generate", () => {
   220	  it("runs a tiny split end to end with seed variants only for qualify families", () => {
   221	    const p = tinyProtocol();
   222	    const g = generate(p, p.families.slice(0, 2).map((f) => f.id), true);
   223	    expect(g.results).toHaveLength(2);
   224	    expect(g.variants.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
   225	  }, T);
   226	});

// Shared builders for the prediction-envelope tests: synthetic envelopes with no simulation, and a tiny protocol for the CPU fixtures.
import { defaultConfig } from "@bl/schema";
import { sealEnvelope, standardMenu, type CensusPoint, type Frame, type PredictionEnvelope, type TaskOp } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
import { draftProtocol, type Protocol } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";

export function censusSeries(start: number, slope: number, n = 31, every = 10): CensusPoint[] {
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ step: i * every, livingCells: 100, freeNutrient: 50, mass: start + slope * i * every }));
}
export function frames(): Frame[] {
  const mass = new Array(64).fill(0);
  mass[0] = 5;
  return [{ step: 300, side: 8, mass }];
}
export function synthEnv(op: TaskOp, o: { familyId?: string; historyId?: string; caseId?: string; census?: CensusPoint[]; delta?: number; horizon?: number } = {}): PredictionEnvelope {
  const cfg = defaultConfig({ tileW: 32, tileH: 32, kernelRadius: 4, seed: 1 });
  return sealEnvelope({
    version: 1,
    caseId: o.caseId ?? "c-test",
    historyId: o.historyId ?? `${o.familyId ?? "fA"}:s1`,
    familyId: o.familyId ?? "fA",
    ruleVersion: 1,
    config: cfg,
    census: o.census ?? censusSeries(1000, 0),
    frames: frames(),
    menu: standardMenu(cfg, 60, 4096, 6),
    task: { subject: "world", op, delta: o.delta ?? 10, readout: "bodyMass", forkStep: 300, horizon: o.horizon ?? 200 },
  });
}
export const LESION: TaskOp = { kind: "lesion", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4 };
export const FEED: TaskOp = { kind: "feed", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4, amount: 64 };
export const DRAIN: TaskOp = { kind: "drain", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4, amount: 64 };
export const SHAM: TaskOp = { kind: "none" };

/** A tiny protocol: 6 short families, 2 mechanisms' worth of work per family, short windows. minEffect and devPrior are set. */
export function tinyProtocol(): Protocol {
  const p = draftProtocol();
  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 6);
  delete p.cohorts;
  p.nDev = 3;
  p.nQualify = 2;
  p.window = { forkStep: 40, delta: 5, horizon: 30, censusEvery: 10, frameSteps: [20, 40] };
  p.mechanisms = p.mechanisms.filter((m) => ["feedOccupied", "lesionOccupied", "sham"].includes(m.id));
  p.proposers.numericTrend.window = 2;
  p.minEffect = 10;
  p.devPrior = { up: 200, down: 300, none: 500 };
  p.frozen = true;
  p.donors = {};
  return p;
}

import { buildOutputs, type Built } from "../lib/prediction/output.ts";
import { generate, splitPlanOf } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
import { donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";

let cached: { p: Protocol; built: Built } | undefined;
/** A complete tiny pipeline (4 families, 3 mechanisms) built once per test file. */
export function tinyPipeline(): { p: Protocol; built: Built } {
  if (cached) return cached;
  const p = tinyProtocol();
  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 4);
  p.nDev = 2;
  p.nQualify = 1;
  p.donors = donorsFor(splitPlanOf(p), p.salt);
  const gen = generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true);
  cached = { p, built: buildOutputs(p, gen) };
  return cached;
}

+## Deviation 3: the `rising` cohort (PLAN.md Amendment 2)
+
+The first delivered run could not discriminate the baselines: every fixture world declined in body mass, so `numericTrend` and
+`shuffledEvidence` both said "down" on all 144 cases and the trend-confound case never appeared. Amendment 2 adds eight families
+(f24..f31: spots light, nutrient 50, founders 1,2,3,4,5,2,3,4, seeds 5024..5031) whose body mass rises through the observed window
+with no intervention. The recipe was fixed by a probe that read only unperturbed mass trajectories (not a mechanism outcome, label,
+threshold or score). The original 24 families keep their dev/test/qualify assignment (each cohort is split by its own hash ranking);
+the cohort adds 4 dev and 4 test families. `minEffect` was not retuned: the Amendment 1 rule on the unchanged qualify tallies again
+gives 25 (21 of 24 matching; 19, 19, 18 at 50, 100, 200). `devPrior` is recomputed over the 16 dev families:
+up 135 / down 271 / none 594. Because the original dev and test families had already been inspected, this regeneration is
+**EXPLORATORY** (`meta.json`), not a held-out confirmation. Cost: the dev stage took about 3 minutes and the full run about 6.3
+minutes on one core (125 M cell-steps).

-  it is not run on, serialised for or shown on test cases (fix for review finding 7).

 ## Limits the data impose (read before citing any comparison)
 
-1. **numericTrend and shuffledEvidence are degenerate.** Both predict "down" on all 144 cases because every fixture world is
-   declining in body mass over the observed window (`comparison.md`, "Predicted class on the test split"). They therefore tie
-   exactly and the run does **not** show the harness separating an evidence-using rule from its shuffled control. What is
-   demonstrated instead, by unit test (`prediction-proposers.test.ts`, "opposite trends"), is that the two rules do differ on a
-   rising/falling pair: `numericTrend` says up on a rising census and down on a falling one, and `shuffledEvidence`, handed the
-   other family's census, says the opposite. The question "does a real evidence-using rule beat its shuffled control" is open.
-2. **The before/after trend confound does not appear in these fixtures.** No case has a treated value above its fork value
-   (0/144), so the naive before/after rule never says "up" on the sham or low-dose cases. The confound arithmetic is verified on
-   a synthetic rising set (`trendConfoundOf` test) only.
+1. The rising cohort is one recipe (spots light, nutrient 50) at one fork step and horizon. It supplies rising baselines and the
+   confound case; it is not a sample of rising worlds. Several of its mechanisms are null because the worlds are saturated, so
+   it also shifts the label mix toward none (test none 57 of 96).
+2. 16 dev and 16 test families (4 + 4 of them rising) is a harness check; intervals are whole-family bootstraps and are descriptive.
 3. Fixtures are 32x32 CPU worlds with fixed mechanisms; the oracle, `v0Rules` and `fixedRule` differences largely reflect that
    `v0Rules` and `fixedRule` encode the operation kind. Nothing is claimed about production presets or any model.
-4. 12 test families is a harness check; intervals are whole-family bootstraps and are descriptive.
+4. The test families were inspected before this regeneration (the first run's comparison was read), and the rising cohort was
+   written after seeing that run's degeneracy. No fresh held-out family exists yet; a confirmation needs fresh families recorded as a dated amendment.

    31	export type Action = "feed" | "drain" | "lesion" | "control";
    32	export type Direction = "up" | "down";
    33	
    34	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35	// Canonical JSON and identity.
    36	
    37	/** Canonical JSON: keys sorted (UTF-16 order) at every depth, no whitespace, numbers as JSON.stringify writes them. Throws on a non-finite number, undefined, or a non-plain object. */
    38	export function canonical(v: unknown, path = "$"): string {
    39	  if (v === null || typeof v === "boolean" || typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);
    40	  if (typeof v === "number") {
    41	    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error(`canonical: ${path} is not a finite number`);
    42	    return JSON.stringify(v);
    43	  }
    44	  if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map((x, i) => canonical(x, `${path}[${i}]`)).join(",")}]`;
    45	  if (typeof v === "object" && (Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === Object.prototype || Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === null)) {
    46	    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    47	    return `{${Object.keys(o)
    48	      .sort()
    49	      .map((k) => {
    50	        if (o[k] === undefined) throw new Error(`canonical: ${path}.${k} is undefined`);
    51	        return `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(o[k], `${path}.${k}`)}`;
    52	      })
    53	      .join(",")}}`;
    54	  }
    55	  throw new Error(`canonical: ${path} has unsupported type ${typeof v}`);
    56	}
    57	const sha = (text: string): string => sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(text));
    58	/** SHA-256 hex of a text (additive export for tools/lib/prediction, which hashes its envelopes the same way). */
    59	export const sha256Text = sha;
    60	export const bodySha256 = (body: ProposalBody): string => sha(canonical(body));
    61	export const proposalId = (body: ProposalBody): string => bodySha256(body).slice(0, 16);
    62	/** The SHA-256 of a run spec's canonical JSON: two rings that propose the same run are one proposal (the note, "Which rings"). */
    63	export const specHash = (spec: RunSpec): string => sha(canonical(spec));
    64

    66	// The notes file (tools/notebook.ts's .notes.json): only the fields the proposer uses, each checked.
    67

    69	  signal: string;

    71	  Z: number;
    72	  W: number;
    73	  startStep: number;
    74	  endStep: number;

    77	  median: number;

// The model seam (PLAN.md, section 4): a typed adapter interface for a future model proposer, with no implementation, no network
// import and nothing registered. Raw model output is routed through the host validator and is never repaired. Unrun in this slice.
import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
import { validatePrediction, type Prediction, type Validation } from "./validate.ts";

export interface ModelContext {
  /** A stable label for the run (model name, prompt version); recorded in meta, never scored. */
  runLabel: string;
}
export interface ModelProposerAdapter {
  id: string;
  /** Returns the raw output: a JSON string or an object. It must be derived from the envelope only. */
  propose(env: PredictionEnvelope, ctx: ModelContext): Promise<unknown>;
}

/** Adapters registered for a run. Empty by design: this slice runs no model. */
export const REGISTERED_ADAPTERS: readonly ModelProposerAdapter[] = Object.freeze([]);

export interface Adapted {
  prediction: Prediction | null;
  validation: Validation;
}

/** Raw output (a JSON string or an object) through the validator. An invalid output yields no prediction and its errors. */
export function adaptModelOutput(raw: unknown, env: PredictionEnvelope): Adapted {
  let value = raw;
  if (typeof raw === "string") {
    try {
      value = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      return { prediction: null, validation: { ok: false, errors: ["output is not JSON"] } };
    }
  }
  const validation = validatePrediction(value, env);
  return { prediction: validation.ok ? (value as Prediction) : null, validation };
}

      --deny-import[=VALUE...]  Deny importing from remote hosts. Optionally specify denied IP addresses and host names, with ports as necessary.
     1	// The source-closure hash covers the transitive local imports of the harness, not a hand-picked list.
     2	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
     4	import { sourceClosure, sourceClosureHash } from "../lib/prediction/closure.ts";
     5	
     6	const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
     7	
     8	describe("sourceClosure", () => {
     9	  const files = sourceClosure(root, ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"]);
    10	  it("includes the executable dependencies the earlier hand-picked list missed", () => {
    11	    for (const f of ["packages/schema/src/world.ts", "packages/schema/src/accounting.ts", "packages/schema/src/presets.ts", "packages/schema/src/genome.ts", "packages/schema/src/kernel.ts", "packages/sim-ref/src/step.ts", "packages/sim-ref/src/cells.ts", "tools/lib/report-html/page.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/closure.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts", "tools/lib/propose.ts"]) expect(files, f).toContain(f);
    12	  });
    13	  it("is sorted, repo-relative and free of node_modules", () => {
    14	    expect([...files].sort()).toEqual(files);
    15	    for (const f of files) {
    16	      expect(f.startsWith("/")).toBe(false);
    17	      expect(f).not.toContain("node_modules");
    18	    }
    19	  });
    20	});
    21	
    22	describe("sourceClosureHash", () => {
    23	  const h = sourceClosureHash(root, ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"]);
    24	  it("covers the resolver configuration and names the external specifiers", () => {
    25	    expect(h.config).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["deno.json", "package.json"]));
    26	    expect(h.externals).toContain("node:fs");
    27	    expect(h.externals).toContain("fflate");
    28	    expect(h.sha256).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    29	  });
    30	  it("is stable across calls", () => {
    31	    expect(sourceClosureHash(root, ["tools/predict.ts", "tools/lib/prediction/model-seam.ts"]).sha256).toBe(h.sha256);
    32	  });
    33	});
